let transitioning=false;

const EN=[],PR=[],PT=[],LOOT=[];
const PLAYER_CANNONBALLS=[]; // V126: dwarf shots; separate from hostile boss projectiles

function gnomeFireCannonball(){
  // Select ONCE at the moment of the shot. No target search is done in the
  // projectile update loop.
  let target=null;
  if(P.tid!=null){
    target=EN.find(function(e){return e&&e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0&&targetIsValid(e)})||null;
    if(target){
      const edge=(target.sz&&target.sz>30)?Math.max(0,(target.sz-30)*.4):0;
      if(Math.hypot(target.x-P.x,target.y-P.y)>playerBasicRange()+edge)target=null;
    }
  }
  if(!target)target=findNearBasic();
  P.tid=target?target.id:null;
  if(!target)return false;

  const dx=target.x-P.x,dy=target.y-P.y;
  const dist=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  const speed=7;

  P.face=dx<0?-1:1;
  P.meleeAng=ang;
  P.shootT=1;
  P.recoil=0;

  const muzzleX=P.x+dx/dist*24;
  const muzzleY=P.y-7+dy/dist*10;
  PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.push({
    x:muzzleX,
    y:muzzleY,
    vx:dx/dist*speed,
    vy:dy/dist*speed,
    remaining:dist,
    target:target
  });

  // Very small game-layer muzzle kick so firing while running still reads
  // clearly without adding effects into the sprite sheet.
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
    PT.push({
      x:muzzleX,y:muzzleY,
      vx:dx/dist*(1.2+i*.45)+(Math.random()-.5)*.6,
      vy:dy/dist*(1.2+i*.45)+(Math.random()-.5)*.6,
      life:6,ml:6,sz:1.5+i*.45,
      col:i===0?'#ffd36a':'#c47a32'
    });
  }
  return true;
}
function updatePlayerCannonballs(){
  // Extremely small loop: move each ball toward the coordinates captured at
  // fire time. At arrival, use the game's already proven damage routine.
  for(let i=PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const b=PLAYER_CANNONBALLS[i];
    b.x+=b.vx;
    b.y+=b.vy;
    b.remaining-=Math.hypot(b.vx,b.vy);

    if(b.remaining<=0){
      const t=b.target;
      if(t&&t.hp>0){
        try{
          applyBasicAttackHit(t);
        }catch(_){
          const raw=Math.max(1,Math.floor(P.atk||12)-(t.def||0));
          t.hp=Math.max(0,t.hp-raw);
          t.flash=6;t.aggro=true;
        }
      }
      PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.splice(i,1);
    }
  }
}
function drawPlayerCannonballs(){
  const _vw=cv.width/cameraZoom(),_vh=cv.height/cameraZoom();
  for(const b of PLAYER_CANNONBALLS){
    const x=b.x-cam.x,y=b.y-cam.y;
    if(x<-24||y<-24||x>_vw+24||y>_vh+24)continue;
    cx.save();

    // Small but clearly readable cannonball on a phone screen.
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(214,125,42,.30)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x,y,10,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.fillStyle='#24272b';
    cx.strokeStyle='#d68a3d';
    cx.lineWidth=2;
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x,y,7,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();cx.stroke();

    cx.fillStyle='rgba(255,221,155,.75)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x-2,y-2,2,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.restore();
  }
}

// === V141 ARCHER BASIC ARROW ==============================================
const PLAYER_ARROWS=[];

function archerFireArrow(){
  let target=null;

  // Prefer the target already locked by Smart Attack.
  if(P.tid!=null){
    target=EN.find(function(e){
      return e&&e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0&&targetIsValid(e);
    })||null;

    if(target){
      const range=playerBasicRange();
      if(P.scene==='clanboss1'&&target.isClanBoss){
        if(clanBossDistanceFromBody(target,P.x,P.y)>range)target=null;
      }else{
        const edge=(target.sz&&target.sz>30)?Math.max(0,(target.sz-30)*.4):0;
        if(Math.hypot(target.x-P.x,target.y-P.y)>range+edge)target=null;
      }
    }
  }

  if(!target)target=findNearBasic();
  P.tid=target?target.id:null;
  if(!target)return false;

  const dx=target.x-P.x,dy=target.y-P.y;
  const dist=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  const speed=12;

  P.face=dx<0?-1:1;
  P.meleeAng=ang;
  P.shootT=1;
  P.recoil=0;

  // Start near the bow hand / chest rather than from the character's feet.
  const startX=P.x+Math.cos(ang)*20;
  const startY=P.y-18+Math.sin(ang)*8;

  PLAYER_ARROWS.push({
    x:startX,y:startY,
    vx:Math.cos(ang)*speed,
    vy:Math.sin(ang)*speed,
    remaining:dist,
    target:target,
    ang:ang
  });

  // Tiny bow release read; deliberately light, no glow spam.
  PT.push({
    x:startX,y:startY,
    vx:-Math.cos(ang)*.7,vy:-Math.sin(ang)*.7,
    life:5,ml:5,sz:1.5,col:'#d8c18b'
  });
  return true;
}

function updatePlayerArrows(){
  for(let i=PLAYER_ARROWS.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const a=PLAYER_ARROWS[i];
    a.x+=a.vx;
    a.y+=a.vy;
    a.remaining-=Math.hypot(a.vx,a.vy);

    if(a.remaining<=0){
      const t=a.target;
      if(t&&t.hp>0){
        try{
          applyBasicAttackHit(t);
        }catch(_){
          const raw=Math.max(1,Math.floor(P.atk||12)-(t.def||0));
          t.hp=Math.max(0,t.hp-raw);
          t.flash=6;t.aggro=true;
        }

        // Small pixel impact only.
        for(let j=0;j<3;j++){
          PT.push({
            x:t.x+(Math.random()-.5)*5,
            y:t.y-8+(Math.random()-.5)*5,
            vx:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,
            vy:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,
            life:7,ml:7,sz:1.3+Math.random(),
            col:j===0?'#e9e1c6':'#8a6b42'
          });
        }
      }
      PLAYER_ARROWS.splice(i,1);
    }
  }
}

function drawPlayerArrows(){
  const _vw=cv.width/cameraZoom(),_vh=cv.height/cameraZoom();
  for(const a of PLAYER_ARROWS){
    const x=a.x-cam.x,y=a.y-cam.y;
    if(x<-28||y<-28||x>_vw+28||y>_vh+28)continue;

    cx.save();
    cx.translate(x,y);
    cx.rotate(a.ang);

    // Pixel-like wooden shaft.
    cx.fillStyle='#b98b55';
    cx.fillRect(-10,-1,16,2);

    // Pale metal arrow head.
    cx.fillStyle='#d9dde0';
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(10,0);
    cx.lineTo(5,-3);
    cx.lineTo(5,3);
    cx.closePath();
    cx.fill();

    // Small dark-green fletching matching the ranger palette.
    cx.fillStyle='#556b3b';
    cx.fillRect(-11,-3,4,2);
    cx.fillRect(-11,1,4,2);

    cx.restore();
  }
}

// === V204 NICKNAME ABOVE PLAYER ===========================================
function ppaPlayerNickname(){
  try{
    var n=(typeof INV!=='undefined'&&INV&&String(INV.playerName||'').trim())
      ||String(window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||'').trim();
    return n||'';
  }catch(_){return String(window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||'').trim()}
}
function ppaPlayerVisualTopScreenY(){
  var sy=P.y-cam.y;
  var _sv=P.scene==='clansiege'?CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE:1;
  var bob=Math.sin(P.bob)*(playerUsesGnomeSprites()?2.0:3)*_sv;
  var dh,dy;
  if(playerUsesGnomeSprites()){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*GNOME_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(113/128)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesArcherSprites()){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*ARCHER_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(184/224)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&ASSASSIN_ANIM[P.anim]){
    var sc=(P.anim==='idle')?ASSASSIN_IDLE_DRAW_SCALE:(P.anim==='attack'?ASSASSIN_ATTACK_DRAW_SCALE:ASSASSIN_RUN_DRAW_SCALE);
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*sc,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(156/192)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesTankSprites()&&TANK_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*TANK_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(232/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&BERSERKER_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*BERSERKER_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(232/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesPriestSprites()&&PRIEST_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*PRIEST_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(236/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesMageSprites()&&MAGE_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*MAGE_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(236/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesPaladinSprites()&&PALADIN_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*PALADIN_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(234/256)*dh;
  }else{
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*1.4,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-dh;
  }
  return sy+(bob+dy)*_sv;
}
function ppaPlayerClanName(){
  try{
    var c=(typeof CLAN_LOCAL_STATE!=='undefined'&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan)
      ?String(CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan.name||'').trim():'';
    return c;
  }catch(_){return ''}
}
function drawPlayerNickname(){
  var name=ppaPlayerNickname();
  if(!name)return;
  var clan=ppaPlayerClanName();
  var sx=P.x-cam.x;
  var y=ppaPlayerVisualTopScreenY()-9;

  cx.save();
  cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.textBaseline='bottom';
  cx.lineJoin='round';

  if(clan){
    var clanText='['+clan.slice(0,18)+']';
    cx.font='700 8px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
    cx.lineWidth=2.4;
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba(8,12,20,.94)';
    cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.78)';
    cx.shadowBlur=3;
    cx.strokeText(clanText,sx,y-12);
    cx.shadowBlur=0;
    cx.fillStyle='#a9cfff';
    cx.fillText(clanText,sx,y-12);
  }

  cx.font='600 10px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
  cx.lineWidth=2.6;
  cx.strokeStyle='rgba(18,8,5,.92)';
  cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.72)';
  cx.shadowBlur=3;
  cx.strokeText(name.slice(0,18),sx,y);
  cx.shadowBlur=0;
  cx.fillStyle='#f2d39a';
  cx.fillText(name.slice(0,18),sx,y);
  cx.restore();
}
// ==========================================================================

function drawPlayer(){
  const sx=P.x-cam.x,sy=P.y-cam.y;
  const siegeVisualScale=P.scene==='clansiege'?CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE:1;

  // V277: only the player's 8 classes are drawn 10 px smaller.
  // No collision, camera, NPC, mob, AI or combat values are changed.
  const PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX=10;
  const playerClassDrawHeight=(baseHeight)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);
    return Math.max(8,phoneCharacterDrawHeight(baseHeight,siegeVisualScale)-PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX/sc);
  };
  const playerClassBodySize=(baseSize)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);

function ppaPlayerClanName(){
  try{
    var c=(typeof CLAN_LOCAL_STATE!=='undefined'&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan)
      ?String(CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan.name||'').trim():'';
    return c;
  }catch(_){return ''}
}
function drawPlayerNickname(){
  var name=ppaPlayerNickname();
  if(!name)return;
  var clan=ppaPlayerClanName();
  var sx=P.x-cam.x;
  var y=ppaPlayerVisualTopScreenY()-9;

  cx.save();
  cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.textBaseline='bottom';
  cx.lineJoin='round';

  if(clan){
    var clanText='['+clan.slice(0,18)+']';
    cx.font='700 8px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
    cx.lineWidth=2.4;
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba(8,12,20,.94)';
    cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.78)';
    cx.shadowBlur=3;
    cx.strokeText(clanText,sx,y-12);
    cx.shadowBlur=0;
    cx.fillStyle='#a9cfff';
    cx.fillText(clanText,sx,y-12);
  }

  cx.font='600 10px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
  cx.lineWidth=2.6;
  cx.strokeStyle='rgba(18,8,5,.92)';
  cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.72)';
  cx.shadowBlur=3;
  cx.strokeText(name.slice(0,18),sx,y);
  cx.shadowBlur=0;
  cx.fillStyle='#f2d39a';
  cx.fillText(name.slice(0,18),sx,y);
  cx.restore();
}

function drawPlayer(){
  const sx=P.x-cam.x,sy=P.y-cam.y;
  const siegeVisualScale=P.scene==='clansiege'?CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE:1;

  // V277: only the player's 8 classes are drawn 10 px smaller.
  // No collision, camera, NPC, mob, AI or combat values are changed.
  const PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX=10;
  const playerClassDrawHeight=(baseHeight)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);
    return Math.max(8,phoneCharacterDrawHeight(baseHeight,siegeVisualScale)-PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX/sc);
  };
  const playerClassBodySize=(baseSize)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);
    return Math.max(8,phoneCharacterBodySize(baseSize,siegeVisualScale)-PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX/sc);
  };
  const isGnome=playerUsesGnomeSprites();
  const isArcher=playerUsesArcherSprites();
  const isAssassinCustom=playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&!!ASSASSIN_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isTank=playerUsesTankSprites()&&!!TANK_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isBerserker=playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&!!BERSERKER_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isPriest=playerUsesPriestSprites()&&!!PRIEST_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isMage=playerUsesMageSprites()&&!!MAGE_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isPaladin=playerUsesPaladinSprites()&&!!PALADIN_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isCustomDirectional=isGnome||isArcher||isAssassinCustom||isTank||isBerserker||isPriest||isMage||isPaladin;
  const phoneBodyBase=playerClassBodySize(P.sz);
  const visualBody=(isGnome?phoneBodyBase*0.72:phoneBodyBase)*siegeVisualScale;
  const bob=P.scene==='fartzone'?0:Math.sin(P.bob)*(isGnome?2.0:3);

  // Shadow follows the visible character size.
  // Fart Zone: exact feet-to-ground anchor, no visual hovering.
  const playerShadowY=P.scene==='fartzone'?sy+visualBody*0.40:sy+visualBody*0.45;
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.4)';
  cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,playerShadowY,visualBody*0.38,visualBody*0.14,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Dust when running
  if((Math.abs(P.vx)>0.5||Math.abs(P.vy)>0.5)&&Math.random()<0.25)
    PT.push({x:P.x+(Math.random()-.5)*(isGnome?6:12)*siegeVisualScale,y:P.y+visualBody*0.35,
      vx:-P.vx*0.4+(Math.random()-.5),vy:-P.vy*0.2+(Math.random()-.5)*0.5,
      life:10,ml:10,sz:(isGnome?1.5:2)*siegeVisualScale,col:'rgba(120,100,70,0.4)'});

  cx.save();
  cx.translate(sx,sy+bob);
  if(P.scene==='clansiege')cx.scale(CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE,CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE);

  // Legacy lean/recoil is disabled for the dwarf because his approved
  // 4-frame attack already contains the physical movement.
  if(!isCustomDirectional){
    let lean=0;
    if(P.shootT>0)lean=P.face>0?0.12:-0.12;
    const rc=P.recoil*6*(P.face>0?-1:1);
    cx.translate(rc,0);
    cx.rotate(lean);
  }

  const cur=playerAnimDef(P.anim);
  const canonicalDir=isCustomDirectional?playerDir8():0;
  const dirRow=isGnome
    ? GNOME_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
    : (isArcher
        ? ARCHER_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
        : (isAssassinCustom
            ? (P.anim==='run'
                ? ASSASSIN_RUN_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
                : ASSASSIN_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir])
            : (isTank
                ? TANK_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                : (isBerserker
                    ? BERSERKER_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                    : (isPriest
                        ? PRIEST_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                        : (isMage
                            ? MAGE_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                            : (isPaladin
                                ? PALADIN_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                                : (cur.rows ? getDirRow() : 0))))))));
  const useFlip=isGnome
    ? GNOME_FLIP_BY_DIR[canonicalDir]
    : (isArcher
        ? ARCHER_FLIP_BY_DIR[canonicalDir]
        : ((isAssassinCustom||isTank||isBerserker||isPriest||isMage||isPaladin) ? false : (dirRow===2 && P.face>0)));
  const frameIndex=((P.animFrame%cur.frames)+cur.frames)%cur.frames;

  if(cur.img.complete && cur.img.naturalWidth > 0){
    const fh_row=cur.fh;
    const fw_frame=cur.fw;
    const footY=P.sz*0.40;
    const srcY=dirRow*fh_row;

    let dh,dw,dx,dy;
    if(isGnome){
      // Approved dwarf size stays unchanged.
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*GNOME_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualSca
===== drawPlayerNickname #1 @ 97884477 =====
-e.sz*0.75*eliteScale;
      cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.82)';
      cx.fillText(_eliteName,sx+1,_nameY+1);
      cx.fillStyle=e.isDungeonElite?'#ffd76a':'#ffdd88';
      cx.fillText(_eliteName,sx,_nameY);
      cx.textAlign='left';
    }
  }

  // Crystal projectiles from the world boss.
  if(P.scene==='worldboss')drawWorldCrystalProjectiles();

  // Boss fireballs
  for(const f of PR){
    const fx=f.x-cam.x,fy=f.y-cam.y;
    const fg=cx.createRadialGradient(fx,fy,0,fx,fy,15);
    fg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,240,180,0.95)');
    fg.addColorStop(0.4,'rgba(255,140,40,0.75)');
    fg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,60,0,0)');
    cx.fillStyle=fg;cx.beginPath();cx.arc(fx,fy,15,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  }

  // Online players from Realtime Presence (same scene only).
  drawOnlinePlayers();

  // Equipped visuals.
  updatePetFollower();
  drawPetFollower();

  // Wings and cloak stay as equipment/craft items,
  // but are NOT rendered in the world.
  // Player is always rendered in the normal world pass.
  // The old reward-only late-render branch could make the hero invisible
  // between Titan death and personal-chest pickup on some mobile browsers.
  drawPlayer();

  // Player projectiles are rendered above world sprites so shots stay readable.
  try{drawPlayerCannonballs()}catch(_){}
  try{drawPlayerArrows()}catch(_){}

  // Artifact floats above/beside the character.
  drawEquippedArtifact();

  // V104 depth for the original Phoenix and all ordinary dungeon/arena mobs.
  // When the player walks behind their ground shadow, their sprite body is redrawn on top.
  ppaDrawWorldBodyOccluders();

  // V89 depth sorting for the large clan boss.
  // If the player's feet are behind the boss, the boss body covers the player.
  if(P.scene==='clanboss1'){
    const _depthBoss=clanBossCombatTarget();
    if(_depthBoss)drawClanBossBodyOccluder(_depthBoss);
  }

  // World boss depth: player disappears behind the Titan when walking behind its body.
  if(P.scene==='worldboss'){
    const _worldDepthBoss=worldCrystalBossEntity();
    if(_worldDepthBoss)drawWorldCrystalBossOccluder(_worldDepthBoss);
  }

  // V204: small frameless character name follows the hero in world space.
  try{drawPlayerNickname()}catch(_){}

  drawMinimap();

  // Return to physical screen coordinates for screen-space effects.
  cx.restore();

  // Vignette — cached until the canvas size changes.
  cx.fillStyle=ppaGetVignetteGradient();cx.fillRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
}

  }
  // Boss hall marker
  m.fillStyle='#7a1a0a';
  m.fillRect(Math.floor(DG_BOSS_IMG[0]/24)-3,Math.floor(DG_BOSS_IMG[1]/24)-3,7,7);
})();
let __v218MinimapAt=0;
function drawMinimap(){
  const mm=document.getElementById('mmap');
  if(P.scene!=='dungeon'){
    __v218MinimapAt=0;
    if(mm.style.display!=='none')mm.style.display='none';
    return;
  }
  mm.style.display='block';
  const now=(typeof performance!=='undefined'&&performance.now)?performance.now():Date.now();
  if(now-__v218MinimapAt<320)return;
  __v218MinimapAt=now;
  const m=mm.getContext('2d');
  m.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
  m.clearRect(0,0,200,133);
  m.drawImage(mmBase,0,0,200,133);
  const sxr=200/SCENES.dungeon.w,syr=133/SCENES.dungeon.h;
  // Viewport rectangle respects the dungeon camera zoom.
  const z=cameraZoom(),vw=cv.width/z,vh=cv.height/z;
  m.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,0.4)';m.lineWidth=1;
  m.strokeRect(cam.x*sxr,cam.y*syr,vw*sxr,vh*syr);
  // Mobs + boss
  for(const e of EN){
    if(e.isBoss){m.fillStyle='#ff3311';m.fillRect(e.x*sxr-2.5,e.y*syr-2.5,5,5)}
    else{m.fillStyle='#ffaa33';m.fillRect(e.x*sxr-1.5,e.y*syr-1.5,3,3)}
  }
  // Player (blinking)
  const blink=Math.sin(Date.now()*0.008)>-0.3;
  if(blink){m.fillStyle='#ffff44';m.fillRect(P.x*sxr-2,P.y*syr-2,4,4)}
}

function drawNPC(){
  const t=Date.now()*0.003;
  const groundDrop=12; // visually seat every NPC on the town floor
  for(const npc of ((SCENES[P.scene]&&SCENES[P.scene].npcs)||[])){
    if(npc.hidden)continue;
    const nx=npc.x-cam.x,ny=npc.y-cam.y;
    if(nx<-220||ny<-260||nx>cv.width+220||ny>cv.height+220)continue;
    const gy=ny+groundDrop;
    const glow=Math.sin(t*3+npc.x*0.001)*0.12+0.88;

    // Dark contact shadow so the NPC base visibly touches the ground.
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.30)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(nx,gy+15,47,13,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    // Small interaction glow directly on the ground.
    const g=cx.createRadialGradient(nx,gy,0,nx,gy,70);
    g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,190,70,'+(0.18*glow)+')');
    g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,190,70,0)');
    cx.fillStyle=g;cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(nx,gy+8,70,28,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    // Actual supplied NPC art, lowered to a common ground baseline.
    if(npc.img&&npc.img.complete&&npc.img.naturalWidth>0){
      const dh=npc.id==='dungeon'?126:(npc.id==='fartzone_guide'?90:112);
      const dw=dh*(npc.img.naturalWidth/npc.img.naturalHeight);
      cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
      cx.drawImage(npc.img,nx-dw/2,gy-dh+18,dw,dh);
    }else if(npc.id==='clan'){
      // Temporary in-game pixel silhouette until the dedicated Clan Master sprite is approved.
      cx.save();
      cx.translate(Math.round(nx),Math.round(gy));
      cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
      cx.fillStyle='#151116';cx.fillRect(-22,-84,44,70);
      cx.fillStyle='#2b1718';cx.fillRect(-17,-72,34,50);
      cx.fillStyle='#9a6b2d';cx.fillRect(-3,-63,6,34);
      cx.fillStyle='#c89642';cx.fillRect(-8,-50,16,4);
      cx.fillStyle='#241b18';cx.fillRect(-14,-101,28,23);
      cx.fillStyle='#0a090b';cx.fillRect(-10,-96,20,18);
      cx.fillStyle='#d0a452';cx.fillRect(-4,-91,3,3);cx.fillRect(2,-91,3,3);
      cx.fillStyle='#7c5327';cx.fillRect(-26,-72,8,48);cx.fillRect(18,-72,8,48);
      cx.fillStyle='#c8953c';cx.font='bold 19px serif';cx.textAlign='center';cx.fillText('⚜',0,-28);
      cx.restore();
    }

    cx.fillStyle='rgba(255,220,120,'+glow+')';
    cx.font='bold 12px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';
    cx.fillText('▶ '+npc.label,nx,gy-158);
    cx.text

  else{cx.fillStyle=c;cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy,6,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill()}cx.restore();
}

function ppaPlayerNickname(){
  try{
    var n=(typeof INV!=='undefined'&&INV&&String(INV.playerName||'').trim())
      ||String(window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||'').trim();
    return n||'';
  }catch(_){return String(window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||'').trim()}
}
function ppaPlayerVisualTopScreenY(){
  var sy=P.y-cam.y;
  var _sv=P.scene==='clansiege'?CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE:1;
  var bob=Math.sin(P.bob)*(playerUsesGnomeSprites()?2.0:3)*_sv;
  var dh,dy;
  if(playerUsesGnomeSprites()){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*GNOME_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(113/128)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesArcherSprites()){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*ARCHER_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(184/224)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&ASSASSIN_ANIM[P.anim]){
    var sc=(P.anim==='idle')?ASSASSIN_IDLE_DRAW_SCALE:(P.anim==='attack'?ASSASSIN_ATTACK_DRAW_SCALE:ASSASSIN_RUN_DRAW_SCALE);
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*sc,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(156/192)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesTankSprites()&&TANK_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*TANK_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(232/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&BERSERKER_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*BERSERKER_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(232/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesPriestSprites()&&PRIEST_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*PRIEST_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(236/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesMageSprites()&&MAGE_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*MAGE_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(236/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesPaladinSprites()&&PALADIN_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*PALADIN_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(234/256)*dh;
  }else{
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*1.4,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-dh;
  }
  return sy+(bob+dy)*_sv;
}

function worldBossLootUpdate(){
  if(!WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.active)return;
  const now=Date.now();

  if(P.scene!=='worldboss'){
    worldBossFallbackReward();
    return;
  }
  if(now>=WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.expiresAt){
    worldBossFallbackReward();
    return;
  }

  for(const c of WORLD_BOSS_CHESTS){
    const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(now-c.born)/720));
    const ease=1-Math.pow(1-t,3);
    c.x=c.sx+(c.tx-c.sx)*ease;
    c.y=c.sy+(c.ty-c.sy)*ease;
    c.bob+=0.08;

    // Сундуки персональные: пет их не подбирает.
    if(t>=0.92&&Math.hypot(P.x-c.x,P.y-c.y)<55){
      worldBossGrantChestReward(c);
      break;
    }
  }
}
function drawWorldBossPersonalChests(){
  if(!WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.active||P.scene!=='worldboss')return;
  const now=Date.now();
  const _wbViewW=cv.width/cameraZoom();
  for(const c of WORLD_BOSS_CHESTS){
    const sx=c.x-cam.x;
    const sy=c.y-cam.y+Math.sin(c.bob)*3;
    const ancient=c.rarity==='ancient',rare=c.rarity==='rare';
    const edge=ancient?'#d999ff':(rare?'#6bbaff':'#e0b56c');
    const body=ancient?'#613875':(rare?'#234d72':'#6b4528');
    const lid=ancient?'#9b62bd':(rare?'#3d7aa6':'#95683a');

    cx.save();
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.38)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+13,19,6,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.globalAlpha=.18;
    cx.fillStyle=edge;
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy,28,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
    cx.globalAlpha=1;

    cx.fillStyle=body;cx.strokeStyle=edge;cx.lineWidth=2;
    cx.fillRect(sx-16,sy-5,32,19);cx.strokeRect(sx-16,sy-5,32,19);
    cx.fillStyle=lid;cx.fillRect(sx-17,sy-12,34,10);cx.strokeRect(sx-17,sy-12,34,10);
    cx.fillStyle=edge;cx.fillRect(sx-3,sy-3,6,9);
    cx.restore();
  }

  // Небольшой таймер выбора.
  const sec=Math.max(0,Math.ceil((WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.expiresAt-now)/1000));
  cx.save();
  cx.font='bold 12px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.fillStyle='#d9efff';
  cx.fillText('ВЫБЕРИ 1 СУНДУК · '+sec+' сек',_wbViewW/2,36);
  cx.textAlign='left';cx.restore();
}

// V178: independent post-kill heartbeat. It is intentionally separate from
// the main update() chain so one unrelated combat/UI exception cannot strand
// the player or the personal reward phase after the Titan dies.
let WORLD_BOSS_POSTKILL_HEARTBEAT=null;
function worldBossEnsurePostKillHeartbeat(){
  if(WORLD_BOSS_POSTKILL_HEARTBEAT)return;
  WORLD_BOSS_POSTKILL_HEARTBEAT=setInterval(function(){
    try{
      if(!WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.active){
        clearInterval(WORLD_BOSS_POSTKILL_HEARTBEAT);
        WORLD_BOSS_POSTKILL_HEARTBEAT=null;
        return;
      }
      if(P.scene!=='worldboss'){worldBossFallbackReward();return}
      const now=Date.now();
      if(now>=WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.expiresAt){worldBossFallbackReward();return}
      transitioning=false;
      if(P.hp>0)P.dead=false;
      for(const c of WORLD_BOSS_CHESTS){
        c.x=c.tx;c.y=c.ty;c.bob+=0.05;
        if(Math.hypot(P.x-c.x,P.y-c.y)<58){
          worldBossGrantChestReward(c);
          break;
        }
      }
    }catch(err){
      console.error('V178 world boss heartbeat',err);
    }
  },150);
}
// ============================================================================

function worldCrystalBossResetFx(){
  WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILES.length=0;
  WORLD_CRYSTAL_STOMP_FX=null;
  WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_FX=null;
}
// V177: hard unlock after Crystal Titan death. The killing hit can happen while
// Smart Attack, an attack animation or a captured joystick pointer is active.
// Reset every transient combat/input state before the post-kill chest phase.
function worldBossPostKillUnlock(bossX,bossY){
  try{cancelSmartAttack()}catch(_){}
  P.tid=null;
  attackQueued=false;
  P.attacking=false;P.shootT=0;P.recoil=0;P.runAttackT=0;
  P.anim='idle';P.animFrame=0;P.animTimer=0;
  P.vx=0;P.vy=0;
  // Stop every input pointer that can survive the killing frame on Android.
  try{if(typeof activePointer!=='undefined')activePointer=null}catch(_){}
  try{if(typeof joyPointer!=='undefined')joyPointer=null}catch(_){}
  try{resetJoystick()}catch(_){}
  worldCrystalBossResetFx();
  WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILES.length=0;
  PR.length=0;
  // Never keep the hero buried under the Titan's former body/loot origin.
  const bx=Number(bossX)||WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_POS.x,by=Number(bossY)||WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_POS.y;
  if(Math.hypot(P.x-bx,P.y-by)<125){
    const dirs=[[0,1],[1,0],[-1,0],[0,-1],[.707,.707],[-.707,.707],[.707,-.707],[-.707,-.707]];
    for(let rr=150;rr<=270;rr+=30){
      let placed=false;
      for(const q of dirs){
        const tx=bx+q[0]*rr,ty=by+q[1]*rr;
        if(worldBossCircleWalk(tx,ty,(P.sz||60)*.24)){P.x=tx;P.y=ty;placed=true;break}
      }
      if(placed)break;
    }
  }
  // This scene is not transitioning after a kill; make sure no stale lock survives.
  transitioning=false;
  try{updateUI()}catch(_){}
}
function spawnWorldCrystalBoss(){
  if(typeof worldBossDailyAvailable==='function'&&!worldBossDailyAvailable()){
    worldBossLootReset();worldCrystalBossResetFx();return null;
  }
  worldBossLootReset();
  worldCrystalBossResetFx();
  const now=Date.now();
  const e={
    id:++eid,isBoss:true,isWorldCrystalBoss:true,
    type:{n:'КРИСТАЛЬНЫЙ ТИТАН'},
    x:WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_POS.x,y:WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_POS.y,
    hx:WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_POS.x,hy:WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_POS.y,
    hp:WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_HP,mhp:WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_HP,
    xp:1,def:18,dmg:WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILE_DAMAGE,dmgType:'magic',
    sp:0,sz:142,col:'#6aaeff',face:1,bob:0,flash:0,atkCD:0,
    wbDir:'down',wbState:'idle',wbActionUntil:0,wbAoeImpactAt:0,wbAoePending:false,
    wbNextCrystal:now+WORLD_CRYSTAL_CRYSTAL_MS,
    wbNextAoe:now+worldCrystalBossNextAoeDelay()
  };
  EN.push(e);
  return e;
}

function drawWorldCrystalBossGroundFx(e){
  if(WORLD_CRYSTAL_STOMP_FX&&Date.now()-WORLD_CRYSTAL_STOMP_FX.born>=WORLD_CRYSTAL_STOMP_FX.duration)WORLD_CRYSTAL_STOMP_FX=null;
  if(WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_FX&&Date.now()-WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_FX.born>=WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_FX.duration)WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_FX=null;
  if(WORLD_CRYSTAL_STOMP_FX)drawWorldCrystalFxAtlas(imgWorldCrystalStomp,WORLD_CRYSTAL_STOMP_FX,210);
  if(WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_FX)drawWorldCrystalFxAtlas(imgWorldCrystalAoe,WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_FX,WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_RADIUS*2.08);
}
function drawWorldCrystalBossBody(e,depthOnly){
  if(!e||!imgWorldCrystalBoss.complete||!imgWorldCrystalBoss.naturalWidth)return;
  const sx=e.x-cam.x,sy=e.y-cam.y;
  const ds=WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_DRAW;
  const col=worldCrystalBossDirCol(e.wbDir||'down');
  const row=worldCrystalBossActionRow(e.wbState||'idle');
  cx.save();cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
  cx.drawImage(
    imgWorldCrystalBoss,col*256,row*256,256,256,
    sx-ds/2,sy-ds+28,ds,ds
  );
  cx.restore();
}
function drawWorldCrystalBoss(e){
  const sx=e.x-cam.x,sy=e.y-cam.y;
  drawWorldCrystalBossGroundFx(e);

  // Shadow is also the physical body collision footprint.
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.42)';
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(
    sx,sy+WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_SHADOW.cy,
    WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_SHADOW.rx,WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_SHADOW.ry,
    0,0,Math.PI*2
  );
  cx.fill();

  drawWorldCrystalBossBody(e,false);

  // Lightweight hit flash: simple outline, no canvas filter.
  if(e.flash>0){
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba(150,220,255,.72)';
    cx.lineWidth=3;
    cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+8,105,38,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.stroke();
  }

  const bw=210,bh=10,barY=sy-WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_DRAW+38;
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.76)';cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,barY,bw,bh);
  cx.fillStyle='#3aa8ff';cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,barY,bw*Math.max(0,e.hp/e.mhp),bh);
  cx.strokeStyle='rgba(180,225,255,.8)';cx.lineWidth=1;cx.strokeRect(sx-bw/2,barY,bw,bh);
  cx.fillStyle='#d8f3ff';cx.font='bold 13px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.fillText('❄ КРИСТАЛЬНЫЙ ТИТАН · '+Math.max(0,Math.ceil(e.hp))+' / '+e.mhp,sx,barY-8);
  cx.textAlign='left';
}
function drawWorldCrystalBossOccluder(e){
  if(!e||!worldCrystalBossPlayerIsBehind(e))return;
  drawWorldCrystalBossBody(e,true);
}
function drawWorldCrystalProjectiles(){
  if(!imgWorldCrystalProjectile.complete||!imgWorldCrystalProjectile.naturalWidth)return;
  for(const f of WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILES){
    const sx=f.x-cam.x,sy=f.y-cam.y;
    const fr=Math.floor((Date.now()-f.born)/95)%4;
    const size=54;
    cx.save();
    cx.translate(sx,sy);
    cx.rotate(f.angle+Math.PI/2);
    cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
    cx.drawImage(imgWorldCrystalProjectile,fr*192,0,192,192,-size/2,-size/2,size,size);
    cx.restore();
  }
}

        fartStopAutoMining('⚔ Стражи вернулись — добыча остановлена');
      }else{
        FART_ZONE_STATE.activeMineId=mine.id;
        fartMineTick(mine,dt);
      }
    }
  }

  fartAutoButtonUpdate(false);
}

function drawFartZoneMines(){
  if(P.scene!=='fartzone'||!FART_ZONE_STATE.ready)return;
  const active=FART_ZONE_STATE.activeMineId;
  for(const mine of FART_ZONE_STATE.mines){
    const im=FART_MINE_IMG[mine.art];
    const sx=mine.x-cam.x,sy=mine.y-cam.y;
    if(sx<-100||sy<-100||sx>cv.width/cameraZoom()+100||sy>cv.height/cameraZoom()+100)continue;

    cx.save();
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.34)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+22,31,10,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    // Kept deliberately small — roughly character-sized.
    if(im&&im.complete&&im.naturalWidth){
      const h=82,w=h*(im.naturalWidth/im.naturalHeight);
      cx.drawImage(im,sx-w/2,sy-h*.72,w,h);
    }

    const blocked=fartMineHasLivingGuard(mine);
    cx.textAlign='center';
    cx.font='bold 9px monospace';
    cx.fillStyle=blocked?'rgba(255,120,100,.85)':(active===mine.id?'#a8ff8a':'rgba(235,220,170,.86)');
    const label=mine.type==='gold'?'ЗОЛОТО · 20 000/ч':(mine.type==='ppa'?'PPA · 450/ч':'РЕСУРСЫ');
    cx.fillText(blocked?'ОХРАНЯЕТСЯ':label,sx,sy-47);
    cx.textAlign='left';
    cx.restore();
  }
}

function drawDungeon21BossAoe(e,sx,sy){
  const now=Date.now();
  if(!e.d21AoeFxUntil||now>=e.d21AoeFxUntil)return;
  const left=Math.max(0,e.d21AoeFxUntil-now);
  const total=1050;
  const k=1-left/total;
  const rr=DUNGEON21_BOSS_AOE_RADIUS*(e.d21AoePending?1:(.72+.28*k));
  cx.save();
  // V218: ONLY a thin floor ring. No fill = no green "inflating belly".
  cx.lineWidth=e.d21AoePending?2.2:1.6;
  cx.strokeStyle=e.d21AoePending?'rgba(137,255,82,.82)':'rgba(110,220,55,.55)';
  if(e.d21AoePending)cx.setLineDash([8,6]);
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(sx,sy+72,rr,Math.max(18,rr*.20),0,0,Math.PI*2);
  cx.stroke();
  cx.setLineDash([]);
  cx.restore();
}

// AGGRO_R ~ 1 метр в мировых координатах (1 тайл)
const AGGRO_R=110, LEASH_R=260;
// Individual dungeon aggro radius for all standard mobs.
const DUNGEON_AGGRO_R=200;
function dungeonAggroRadiusFor(e){
  const l=Math.max(1,Number(e&&e.lvl)||1);
  if(l<=2)return 90;
  if(l<=4)return 115;
  if(l<=6)return 140;
  if(l<=10)return 170;
  return DUNGEON_AGGRO_R;
}
function mobAttackCooldown(e){
  const l=Math.max(1,Number(e&&e.lvl)||1);
  if(l<=2)return 85;
  if(l<=4)return 75;
  if(l<=6)return 65;
  if(l<=10)return 55;
  return 50;
}
function mkMob(t,x,y){
  const _mmp=MOB_MANA_BY_LEVEL[t.lvl]||0;
  const _dungeonDefBonus=(typeof P!=='undefined'&&P.scene==='dungeon'&&typeof DUNGEON_MODE!=='undefined'&&DUNGEON_MODE==='1-20')
    ?dungeon1120DefBonus(t.lvl):0;
  const e={id:eid++,type:t,x:x,y:y,hx:x,hy:y,lvl:t.lvl,
    hp:t.hp,mhp:t.hp,mp:_mmp,mmp:_mmp,sp:t.sp,sz:t.sz,col:t.col,dmg:t.atk,dmgType:t.damageType||'physical',def:t.def+_dungeonDefBonus,xp:t.xp,gold:t.gold,
    sprite:DUNGEON_MOB_IMAGES[Math.max(0,Math.min(19,(t.lvl||1)-1))],
    atkCD:0,flash:0,bob:Math.random()*6,aggro:false,edgeChase:false,edgeWait:0};
  return applyApprovedMobAnimation(applyCaveSpiderVariant(applyScavengerSlimeVariant(e)));
}

function drawClanSiegeWorld(){
  if(P.scene!=='clansiege'||!PPA_SIEGE.active)return;
  // Respawn coordinates stay active, but their map markers are intentionally hidden.
  cx.save();
  if(PPA_SIEGE.phase==='castle'||PPA_SIEGE.phase==='won'){
    const age=Math.min(1,(Date.now()-(PPA_SIEGE.castleShownAt||Date.now()))/800);
    cx.globalAlpha=Math.max(.18,age);
    const c=CLAN_SIEGE_CASTLE,x=c.x-cam.x,y=c.y-cam.y;
    if(imgClanSiegeCastle.complete&&imgClanSiegeCastle.naturalWidth>0){
      cx.drawImage(imgClanSiegeCastle,x-c.w/2,y-c.h*.78,c.w,c.h);
    }
  }
  cx.restore();
}
function drawClanSiegeCrystal(e){
  const d=CLAN_SIEGE_CRYSTALS.find(x=>x.key===e.siegeKey)||{};
  const im=CLAN_SIEGE_CRYSTAL_IMG[e.siegeKey],sx=e.x-cam.x,sy=e.y-cam.y;
  const flash=e.flash>0;
  cx.save();
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.38)';cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+23,36,11,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  if(im&&im.complete&&im.naturalWidth>0){
    if(flash){cx.globalAlpha=.55}
    const dh=84,dw=dh*(im.naturalWidth/im.naturalHeight);
    cx.drawImage(im,sx-dw/2,sy-dh+30,dw,dh);
    cx.globalAlpha=1;
  }else{
    cx.fillStyle=e.siegeKey==='red'?'#ff4747':e.siegeKey==='blue'?'#52a9ff':e.siegeKey==='green'?'#51dd83':'#b56cff';
    cx.beginPath();cx.moveTo(sx,sy-37);cx.lineTo(sx+18,sy-6);cx.lineTo(sx,sy+19);cx.lineTo(sx-18,sy-6);cx.closePath();cx.fill();
  }
  const bw=92,bh=6,ratio=Math.max(0,e.hp/e.mhp);
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.78)';cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,sy-53,bw,bh);
  cx.fillStyle=e.siegeKey==='red'?'#ff4949':e.siegeKey==='blue'?'#4da8ff':e.siegeKey==='green'?'#4adb78':'#b15fff';
  cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,sy-53,bw*ratio,bh);
  cx.strokeStyle='#ead7a0';cx.lineWidth=1;cx.strokeRect(sx-bw/2,sy-53,bw,bh);
  cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.font='bold 9px monospace';cx.fillStyle='#f3e2bc';cx.fillText(d.name||e.type.n,sx,sy-64);
  const hpTxt=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(e.hp)).toLocaleString('ru-RU')+' / '+Math.ceil(e.mhp).toLocaleString('ru-RU')+' HP';
  cx.font='bold 8px monospace';

  cx.fillStyle='#ffffff';cx.fillText(hpTxt,sx,sy-41);
  cx.restore();
}

function changeScene(target){
  if(target==='clansiege'){
    const clanKey=(typeof clanSiegeLocalClanKey==='function'?clanSiegeLocalClanKey():'');
    if(!clanKey){
      showPickup('🏰 ЗАМОК ФЕНИКСА ДОСТУПЕН ТОЛЬКО УЧАСТНИКАМ КЛАНА','#ff9b72');
      return;
    }
  }
  if(target==='dungeon'){
    const lv=Math.floor(Number(P&&P.lvl)||1);
    const allowed=(DUNGEON_MODE==='1-20')
      ?(lv>=1&&lv<=20)
      :(DUNGEON_MODE==='21+')
        ?(lv>=21&&lv<=40)
        :(DUNGEON_MODE==='41-60')
          ?(lv>=41&&lv<=60)
          :false;
    if(!allowed){
      showPickup('Подземелье недоступно для текущего уровня','#ff9b72');
      return;
    }
  }
  if(target==='fartzone'&&!fartZoneLevelAllowed()){
    showPickup('Фарт Зона доступна только на уровнях 21–40','#ffb36a');
    return;
  }
  if(P.scene==='clansiege'&&target!=='clansiege')clanSiegeLeaveCleanup();
  if(P.scene==='arena'&&target!=='arena'&&typeof v238ArenaXpEndSession==='function')v238ArenaXpEndSession();
  // V225: leaving a started daily AI challenge is a forfeit.
  if(target!=='pvp1'&&P.scene==='pvp1'&&window.PPA_AI_TRAINING_ACTIVE===true&&typeof v225AiForfeit==='function'){
    v225AiForfeit('Выход из испытания',true);
  }
  // V184: AI opponent exists only in the explicit AI-training mode.
  if(target!=='pvp1')window.PPA_AI_TRAINING_ACTIVE=false;
  if(P.scene==='worldboss'&&target!=='worldboss'&&WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.active&&!WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.claimed){
    worldBossFallbackReward();
  }
  const fade=document.getElementById('fade');
  fade.classList.add('on');
  transitioning=true;
  setTimeout(()=>{
    P.scene=target;
    if(target!=='dungeon')dungeonSpawnToken++;
    const sc=SCENES[target];
    EN.length=0;PR.length=0;PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0;PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0;cancelSmartAttack();worldCrystalBossResetFx();P.tid=null;if(typeof closeMobInfo==='function')closeMobInfo();
    if(target==='arena'){
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      if(typeof v238ArenaXpBeginSession==='function')v238ArenaXpBeginSession();
      P.x=sc.center.x;P.y=sc.center.y;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
      startWave();
    }else if(target==='pvp1'){
      const dsp=PVP_DUEL_SPAWNS[PVP_LOCAL_SIDE][0];
      P.x=dsp.x;P.y=dsp.y;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
      startPvpDuelWarmup();

function drawClanSiegeCrystal(e){
  const d=CLAN_SIEGE_CRYSTALS.find(x=>x.key===e.siegeKey)||{};
  const im=CLAN_SIEGE_CRYSTAL_IMG[e.siegeKey],sx=e.x-cam.x,sy=e.y-cam.y;
  const flash=e.flash>0;
  cx.save();
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.38)';cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+23,36,11,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  if(im&&im.complete&&im.naturalWidth>0){
    if(flash){cx.globalAlpha=.55}
    const dh=84,dw=dh*(im.naturalWidth/im.naturalHeight);
    cx.drawImage(im,sx-dw/2,sy-dh+30,dw,dh);
    cx.globalAlpha=1;
  }else{
    cx.fillStyle=e.siegeKey==='red'?'#ff4747':e.siegeKey==='blue'?'#52a9ff':e.siegeKey==='green'?'#51dd83':'#b56cff';
    cx.beginPath();cx.moveTo(sx,sy-37);cx.lineTo(sx+18,sy-6);cx.lineTo(sx,sy+19);cx.lineTo(sx-18,sy-6);cx.closePath();cx.fill();
  }
  const bw=92,bh=6,ratio=Math.max(0,e.hp/e.mhp);
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.78)';cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,sy-53,bw,bh);
  cx.fillStyle=e.siegeKey==='red'?'#ff4949':e.siegeKey==='blue'?'#4da8ff':e.siegeKey==='green'?'#4adb78':'#b15fff';
  cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,sy-53,bw*ratio,bh);
  cx.strokeStyle='#ead7a0';cx.lineWidth=1;cx.strokeRect(sx-bw/2,sy-53,bw,bh);
  cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.font='bold 9px monospace';cx.fillStyle='#f3e2bc';cx.fillText(d.name||e.type.n,sx,sy-64);
  const hpTxt=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(e.hp)).toLocaleString('ru-RU')+' / '+Math.ceil(e.mhp).toLocaleString('ru-RU')+' HP';
  cx.font='bold 8px monospace';
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.82)';cx.fillText(hpTxt,sx+1,sy-41+1);
  cx.fillStyle='#ffffff';cx.fillText(hpTxt,sx,sy-41);
  cx.restore();
}

function ppaOnlineClassColor(cls){
  cls=String(cls||'').toLowerCase();
  if(cls.includes('маг'))return'#7fa7ff';if(cls.includes('жр')||cls.includes('priest'))return'#f0d88a';
  if(cls.includes('асс')||cls.includes('assassin'))return'#a883d9';if(cls.includes('луч')||cls.includes('archer'))return'#79ba76';
  if(cls.includes('гном')||cls.includes('cannon'))return'#d38b4b';if(cls.includes('бер')||cls.includes('barb'))return'#d5685f';
  if(cls.includes('пал'))return'#e4c45b';if(cls.includes('страж')||cls.includes('tank'))return'#93a5b8';return'#6fb8ff';
}
function ppaOnlineDrawRemote(r,now,nearCount){
  if(!r||!r.hasPos)return false;
  let dt=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,now-(r.lastDrawAt||now)));r.lastDrawAt=now;
  const alpha=1-Math.exp(-dt/105);r.x+=(r.tx-r.x)*alpha;r.y+=(r.ty-r.y)*alpha;
  const sx=r.x-cam.x,sy=r.y-cam.y;
  const vw=cv.width/cameraZoom(),vh=cv.height/cameraZoom();
  if(sx<-90||sy<-120||sx>vw+90||sy>vh+120)return false;
  const phoneScale=(typeof PHONE_REMOTE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE==='number'?PHONE_REMOTE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE:1);
  const siegeScale=(P.scene==='clansiege'&&typeof CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE==='number'?CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE:1);
  const sc=phoneScale*siegeScale;
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.34)';cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+16*sc,17*sc,5.5*sc,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  const body=ppaOnlineClassColor(r.cls);cx.fillStyle=body;cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy-8*sc,16*sc,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  cx.fillStyle='#e9f4ff';cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy-18*sc,7.5*sc,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  const dist=Math.hypot(r.x-P.x,r.y-P.y);
  if(nearCount<=14||dist<360){
    cx.fillStyle='#fff';cx.font='bold 10px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';cx.fillText(String(r.name||'Игрок').slice(0,18),sx,sy-34*sc);
  }
  if(r.mhp>0&&dist<650){
    const bw=36*sc;cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.65)';cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,sy-29*sc,bw,4);
    cx.fillStyle='#47dd78';cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,sy-29*sc,bw*Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(r.hp||0)/r.mhp)),4);
  }
  cx.textAlign='left';return true;
}
function drawOnlinePlayers(){
  const hasLive=PPA_ONLINE.connected&&PPA_ONLINE.remotes.size;const hasDebug=PPA_ONLINE.debugRemotes.size;
  if(!hasLive&&!hasDebug){PPA_ONLINE.drawnLast=0;return}
  const now=(typeof performance!=='undefined'&&performance.now)?performance.now():Date.now();
  const all=[];
  if(hasLive)for(const r of PPA_ONLINE.remotes.values())all.push(r);
  if(hasDebug){
    let k=0;for(const r of PPA_ONLINE.debugRemotes.values()){
      const a=now*.00035+r.seed*1.77,rad=105+(r.seed%9)*34;
      r.tx=P.x+Math.cos(a)*rad;r.ty=P.y+Math.sin(a*.93)*rad;r.hasPos=true;r.hp=r.mhp=100;r.lastNetAt=now;k++;all.push(r);
    }
  }
  const vw=cv.width/cameraZoom(),vh=cv.height/cameraZoom();
  const visible=[];
  for(const r of all){
    if(!r||!r.hasPos)continue;
    const tx=r.tx-cam.x,ty=r.ty-cam.y;
    if(tx<-120||ty<-150||tx>vw+120||ty>vh+150)continue;
    visible.push(r);
  }
  const small=(Math.min(window.innerWidth||cv.width,window.innerHeight||cv.height)<720);
  const cap=small?18:32;
  if(visible.length>cap){visible.sort(function(a,b){return Math.hypot(a.tx-P.x,a.ty-P.y)-Math.hypot(b.tx-P.x,b.ty-P.y)});visible.length=cap}
  let drawn=0;for(const r of visible)if(ppaOnlineDrawRemote(r,now,visible.length))drawn++;
  PPA_ONLINE.drawnLast=drawn;
}
// Local rendering stress tool for release testing. It creates no network traffic.
window.PPA_ONLINE_STRESS=function(count){
  count=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.floor(Number(count)||0)));PPA_ONLINE.debugRemotes.clear();
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++)PPA_ONLINE.debugRemotes.set('stress-'+i,{id:'stress-'+i,name:'BOT '+(i+1),cls:['Маг','Жрец','Ассасин','Лучник','Гном-канонир','Берсерк','Паладин','Страж'][i%8],x:P.x,y:P.y,tx:P.x,ty:P.y,hasPos:true,hp:100,mhp:100,face:1,anim:'walk',lastSeq:0,lastNetAt:0,lastDrawAt:0,seed:i+1});
  return window.PPA_ONLINE_DIAG();
};
window.PPA_ONLINE_STRESS_OFF=function(){PPA_ONLINE.debugRemotes.clear();return window.PPA_ONLINE_DIAG()};
window.PPA_ONLINE_DIAG=function(){return{room:PPA_ONLINE.roomKey||ppaOnlineRoomKey(),connected:PPA_ONLINE.connected,remoteCount:PPA_ONLINE.remotes.size,stressCount:PPA_ONLINE.debugRemotes.size,drawnLast:PPA_ONLINE.drawnLast,sentPackets:PPA_ONLINE.sentPackets,recvPackets:PPA_ONLINE.recvPackets}};
window.addEventListener('beforeunload',function(){
  try{if(PPA_ONLINE.channel)PPA_ONLINE.channel.untrack()}catch(_){ }
  try{if(PPA_ONLINE.supabase&&PPA_ONLINE.channel)PPA_ONLINE.supabase.removeChannel(PPA_ONLINE.channel)}catch(_){ }
});

// === V174 CLASS AI TRAINING ================================================
// Lightweight PvP sparring AI. No particle-heavy skill FX: the opponent uses
// the approved class sprites, movement, numeric buffs/debuffs and short labels.
// Every AI fighter owns all four active skills and all class passives from the
// start. Difficulty represents higher skill ranks + tighter decision making.

};

function v174DrawAiTrainingFighter(e,sx,sy){
  const now=Date.now(),c=V174_AI_CLASS[e.aiClass]||V174_AI_CLASS.assassin;
  const cfg=v174AiSpriteCfg(e),a=cfg.anim,di=v174AiDirIndex(e);
  const bob=Math.sin(e.bob)*2;
  const _aiHidden=(e.aiClass==='assassin'&&now<(e.aiHiddenUntil||0));
  cx.save();
  if(_aiHidden)cx.globalAlpha=.12;
  const aiVisualSize=phoneCharacterBodySize(e.sz,1);
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.42)';cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+aiVisualSize*.45,aiVisualSize*.38,aiVisualSize*.14,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  if(a&&a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0){
    const frame=Math.floor(now/(1000/Math.max(1,a.fps)))%a.frames;
    const row=cfg.rowMap?cfg.rowMap[di]:0;
    const dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(e.sz*cfg.scale,1),dw=dh;
    cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
    if(e.flash>0)cx.filter='brightness(1.65)';
    cx.drawImage(a.img,frame*a.fw,row*a.fh,a.fw,a.fh,sx-dw/2,sy+e.sz*.40-cfg.foot*dh+bob,dw,dh);
    cx.filter='none';
  }else{
    cx.fillStyle='#9d63db';cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy-10,aiVisualSize*.35,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  }
  cx.globalAlpha=1;
  if(_aiHidden&&now>(e.aiSmokeLabelUntil||0)){
    cx.globalAlpha=.22;cx.strokeStyle='#8d8d9a';cx.setLineDash([4,5]);
    cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+e.sz*.35,e.sz*.48,e.sz*.18,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.stroke();
    cx.setLineDash([]);cx.restore();return;
  }
  const bw=Math.max(78,e.sz*1.25),by=sy-e.sz*.86;
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.72)';cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,by,bw,5);
  cx.fillStyle='#d04fff';cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,by,bw*Math.max(0,e.hp/e.mhp),5);
  cx.font='bold 10px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';cx.fillStyle='#e7c8ff';
  cx.fillText('ИИ · '+c.name+' · '+['','I','II','III'][e.aiRank],sx,by-6);

  // Compact skill indicator: small "skill button" + its real name.
  if(now<(e.aiSkillBadgeUntil||0)&&e.aiCastName){
    const idx=Number.isInteger(e.aiSkillIndex)?e.aiSkillIndex:-1;
    const tag=idx>=0?String(idx+1):'A';
    const txt=e.aiCastName==='Обычная атака'?'АТАКА':e.aiCastName;
    const tw=Math.min(150,Math.max(64,cx.measureText(txt).width+35));
    const bx=sx-tw/2,yy=by-31;
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(8,8,10,.86)';cx.fillRect(bx,yy,tw,18);
    cx.strokeStyle=idx>=0?'rgba(255,197,88,.95)':'rgba(215,215,215,.75)';
    cx.lineWidth=1;cx.strokeRect(bx+.5,yy+.5,tw-1,17);
    cx.fillStyle=idx>=0?'#8b4f18':'#454545';cx.fillRect(bx+3,yy+3,13,12);
    cx.fillStyle='#fff2c9';cx.font='bold 8px monospace';cx.fillText(tag,bx+9.5,yy+12);
    cx.fillStyle='#ffd783';cx.font='bold 8px monospace';
    cx.fillText(txt.slice(0,22),bx+19+(tw-22)/2,yy+12);
  }

  // Tiny, cheap hit stroke: ranged attacks visibly connect to the target;
  // melee attacks produce a short slash at the player's feet.
  if(now<(e.aiHitFxUntil||0)){
    const px=P.x-cam.x,py=P.y-cam.y;
    const alpha=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,((e.aiHitFxUntil||0)-now)/170));
    cx.save();cx.globalAlpha=alpha;
    cx.strokeStyle=e.aiHitFxType==='magic'?'#b98cff':'#ffbd62';
    cx.lineWidth=e.aiHitFxCrit?3:2;
    if(c.range>150){
      cx.beginPath();cx.moveTo(sx,sy-e.sz*.12);cx.lineTo(px,py-e.sz*.18);cx.stroke();
    }else{
      cx.beginPath();cx.moveTo(px-13,py-18);cx.lineTo(px+14,py+2);
      cx.moveTo(px+11,py-19);cx.lineTo(px-9,py+3);cx.stroke();
    }
    cx.restore();
  }

  cx.textAlign='left';cx.restore();
}

let __ppaHudNextAt=0;
function update(){
  PPAOnlineTick();
  ppaCleanupDeathRemnants();
  updateConsumableHud(false);
  updatePremiumRegen();
  if(P.dead)return;
  v174AiTickPlayerDebuffs();

  const _smartMove=updateSmartAttackInput();
  // V139: Smart Attack only supplies movement when joystick is neutral.
  // Any live joystick input keeps full manual movement while target lock stays active.
  const _moveX=_smartMove?_smartMove.x:jX;
  const _moveY=_smartMove?_smartMove.y:jY;

  // V174: class-AI crowd control is numeric only (no particle FX).
  const _aiMoveMul=Date.now()<(P.aiRootUntil||0)?0:(Date.now()<(P.aiSlowUntil||0)?Math.max(.30,Number(P.aiSlowMul)||.55):1);
  P.vx=_moveX*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  P.vy=_moveY*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  let nx=P.x+P.vx, ny=P.y+P.vy;

  // Keep in bounds (per scene)
  const sc=SCENES[P.scene];
  nx=Math.max(sc.bounds.minX,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxX,nx));
  ny=Math.max(sc.bounds.minY,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxY,ny));

  if(P.scene==='arena'){
    // Stay on the circular arena floor — no wandering into the black void
    const c=sc.center,rad=460;
    const dx=nx-c.x,dy=ny-c.y,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
    if(dist>rad){nx=c.x+dx/dist*rad;ny=c.y+dy/dist*rad}
  }else if(P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam'){
    const mv=pvpSlide(P.scene,P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
    // Dungeon walls — slide along corridors, never clip through
    const mv=dgSlide(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='clansiege'){
    const mv=clanSiegeSlide(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='clanboss1'){
    const mv=clanBossMoveWithBodyCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='worldboss'){
    const mv=worldBossMoveWithCrystalCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='fartzone'){
    // Exact user-drawn meadow boundary: outside = solid collision.
    // Do not alter town/building hitboxes or other scenes.
    if(!pointInPolygonFartZone(nx,ny)){
      nx=P.x;ny=P.y;
    }
  }else{
    // Solid buildings — can't walk through walls or across rooftops
    const resolved=resolveCollision(nx,ny);
    nx=resolved.x;ny=resolved.y;
  }
  // V104: keep the player's visible foot shadow outside Phoenix/mob shadows.
  // The map/wall collision above remains authoritative; this only adds body bl

    const tw=Math.min(150,Math.max(64,cx.measureText(txt).width+35));
    const bx=sx-tw/2,yy=by-31;
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(8,8,10,.86)';cx.fillRect(bx,yy,tw,18);
    cx.strokeStyle=idx>=0?'rgba(255,197,88,.95)':'rgba(215,215,215,.75)';
    cx.lineWidth=1;cx.strokeRect(bx+.5,yy+.5,tw-1,17);
    cx.fillStyle=idx>=0?'#8b4f18':'#454545';cx.fillRect(bx+3,yy+3,13,12);
    cx.fillStyle='#fff2c9';cx.font='bold 8px monospace';cx.fillText(tag,bx+9.5,yy+12);
    cx.fillStyle='#ffd783';cx.font='bold 8px monospace';
    cx.fillText(txt.slice(0,22),bx+19+(tw-22)/2,yy+12);
  }

  // Tiny, cheap hit stroke: ranged attacks visibly connect to the target;
  // melee attacks produce a short slash at the player's feet.
  if(now<(e.aiHitFxUntil||0)){
    const px=P.x-cam.x,py=P.y-cam.y;
    const alpha=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,((e.aiHitFxUntil||0)-now)/170));
    cx.save();cx.globalAlpha=alpha;
    cx.strokeStyle=e.aiHitFxType==='magic'?'#b98cff':'#ffbd62';
    cx.lineWidth=e.aiHitFxCrit?3:2;
    if(c.range>150){
      cx.beginPath();cx.moveTo(sx,sy-e.sz*.12);cx.lineTo(px,py-e.sz*.18);cx.stroke();
    }else{
      cx.beginPath();cx.moveTo(px-13,py-18);cx.lineTo(px+14,py+2);
      cx.moveTo(px+11,py-19);cx.lineTo(px-9,py+3);cx.stroke();
    }
    cx.restore();
  }

  cx.textAlign='left';cx.restore();
}
// ========================================================================

let __ppaHudNextAt=0;
function update(){
  PPAOnlineTick();
  ppaCleanupDeathRemnants();
  updateConsumableHud(false);
  updatePremiumRegen();
  if(P.dead)return;
  v174AiTickPlayerDebuffs();

  const _smartMove=updateSmartAttackInput();
  // V139: Smart Attack only supplies movement when joystick is neutral.
  // Any live joystick input keeps full manual movement while target lock stays active.
  const _moveX=_smartMove?_smartMove.x:jX;
  const _moveY=_smartMove?_smartMove.y:jY;

  // V174: class-AI crowd control is numeric only (no particle FX).
  const _aiMoveMul=Date.now()<(P.aiRootUntil||0)?0:(Date.now()<(P.aiSlowUntil||0)?Math.max(.30,Number(P.aiSlowMul)||.55):1);
  P.vx=_moveX*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  P.vy=_moveY*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  let nx=P.x+P.vx, ny=P.y+P.vy;

  // Keep in bounds (per scene)
  const sc=SCENES[P.scene];
  nx=Math.max(sc.bounds.minX,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxX,nx));
  ny=Math.max(sc.bounds.minY,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxY,ny));

  if(P.scene==='arena'){
    // Stay on the circular arena floor — no wandering into the black void
    const c=sc.center,rad=460;
    const dx=nx-c.x,dy=ny-c.y,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
    if(dist>rad){nx=c.x+dx/dist*rad;ny=c.y+dy/dist*rad}
  }else if(P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam'){
    const mv=pvpSlide(P.scene,P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
    // Dungeon walls — slide along corridors, never clip through
    const mv=dgSlide(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='clansiege'){
    const mv=clanSiegeSlide(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='clanboss1'){
    const mv=clanBossMoveWithBodyCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='worldboss'){
    const mv=worldBossMoveWithCrystalCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='fartzone'){
    // Exact user-drawn meadow boundary: outside = solid collision.
    // Do not alter town/building hitboxes or other scenes.
    if(!pointInPolygonFartZone(nx,ny)){
      nx=P.x;ny=P.y;
    }
  }else{
    // Solid buildings — can't walk through walls or across rooftops
    const resolved=resolveCollision(nx,ny);
    nx=resolved.x;ny=resolved.y;
  }
  // V104: keep the player's visible foot shadow outside Phoenix/mob shadows.
  // The map/wall collision above remains authoritative; this only adds body blocking.
  if(P.scene==='dungeon'||P.scene==='arena'){
    const _bodyMv=ppaMoveWithWorldBodyCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*.24);
    nx=_bodyMv.x;ny=_bodyMv.y;
  }
  P.x=nx;P.y=ny;
  if(P.scene==='clansiege')clanSiegeUpdateCapture();

  // Portal collision
  if(P.portalCD>0)P.portalCD--;
  if(P.portalCD<=0&&!transitioning&&sc.portal){
    const p=sc.portal;
    if(Math.hypot(P.x-p.x,P.y-p.y)<p.r+20){
      changeScene(p.target);
    }
  }
  // Town teleport -> dungeon (walk into the rune circle)
  if(P.portalCD<=0&&!transitioning&&P.scene==='safe'&&sc.teleport&&sc.teleport.target){
    const tp=sc.teleport;
    if(Math.hypot(P.x-tp.x,P.y-tp.y)<tp.r){
      changeScene(tp.target);
    }
  }
  // 1x1 duel preparation: both players may move, but PvP damage stays locked for 10 seconds.
  updatePvpDuelWarmup();

  // Arena ladder: only a fully cleared stage advances
===== fillText( #12 @ 97868187 =====

  // V89 depth sorting for the large clan boss.
  // If the player's feet are behind the boss, the boss body covers the player.
  if(P.scene==='clanboss1'){
    const _depthBoss=clanBossCombatTarget();
    if(_depthBoss)drawClanBossBodyOccluder(_depthBoss);
  }

  // World boss depth: player disappears behind the Titan when walking behind its body.
  if(P.scene==='worldboss'){
    const _worldDepthBoss=worldCrystalBossEntity();
    if(_worldDepthBoss)drawWorldCrystalBossOccluder(_worldDepthBoss);
  }

  // V204: small frameless character name follows the hero in world space.
  try{drawPlayerNickname()}catch(_){}

  drawMinimap();

  // Return to physical screen coordinates for screen-space effects.
  cx.restore();

  // Vignette — cached until the canvas size changes.
  cx.fillStyle=ppaGetVignetteGradient();cx.fillRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
}

let __v218MinimapAt=0;
function drawMinimap(){
  const mm=document.getElementById('mmap');
  if(P.scene!=='dungeon'){
    __v218MinimapAt=0;
    if(mm.style.display!=='none')mm.style.display='none';
    return;
  }
  mm.style.display='block';
  const now=(typeof performance!=='undefined'&&performance.now)?performance.now():Date.now();
  if(now-__v218MinimapAt<320)return;
  __v218MinimapAt=now;
  const m=mm.getContext('2d');
  m.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
  m.clearRect(0,0,200,133);
  m.drawImage(mmBase,0,0,200,133);
  const sxr=200/SCENES.dungeon.w,syr=133/SCENES.dungeon.h;
  // Viewport rectangle respects the dungeon camera zoom.
  const z=cameraZoom(),vw=cv.width/z,vh=cv.height/z;
  m.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,0.4)';m.lineWidth=1;
  m.strokeRect(cam.x*sxr,cam.y*syr,vw*sxr,vh*syr);
  // Mobs + boss
  for(const e of EN){
    if(e.isBoss){m.fillStyle='#ff3311';m.fillRect(e.x*sxr-2.5,e.y*syr-2.5,5,5)}
    else{m.fillStyle='#ffaa33';m.fillRect(e.x*sxr-1.5,e.y*syr-1.5,3,3)}
  }
  // Player (blinking)
  const blink=Math.sin(Date.now()*0.008)>-0.3;
  if(blink){m.fillStyle='#ffff44';m.fillRect(P.x*sxr-2,P.y*syr-2,4,4)}
}

function drawNPC(){
  const t=Date.now()*0.003;
  const groundDrop=12; // visually seat every NPC on the town floor
  for(
===== fillText( #23 @ 97882450 =====
*.80,baseW,baseH);
      cx.filter='none';cx.globalAlpha=1;
      cx.restore();
    }
    // Other dungeon monster sprites.
    else if(e.sprite && e.sprite.complete && e.sprite.naturalWidth>0){
      // Scavenger slime is exactly about 1/4 of the current player on screen:
      // 22 world px × 1.18 camera = ~26 px. Other monsters keep their old sizes.
      const dh=(isScavengerSlime?25:Math.max(54,Math.min(92,e.sz*2.0)))*eliteScale;
      const widthMul=isScavengerSlime?(e.slimeWidthMul||1):1;
      const dw=dh*(e.sprite.naturalWidth/e.sprite.naturalHeight)*widthMul;
      cx.save();
      cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
      // Tiny hit flash without replacing the sprite silhouette.
      if(e.flash>0){
        cx.globalAlpha=0.72;
        cx.filter='brightness(1.8)';
      }
      if(isScavengerSlime&&e.slimeFlip){
        cx.translate(sx,0);
        cx.scale(-1,1);
        cx.drawImage(e.sprite,-dw/2,sy-dh*0.78+bob,dw,dh);
      }else{
        cx.drawImage(e.sprite,sx-dw/2,sy-dh*0.78+bob,dw,dh);
      }
      cx.filter='none';
      cx.globalAlpha=1;
      cx.restore();
    }else{
      // Fallback only if a sprite failed to load.
      cx.fillStyle=e.flash>0?'#ffffff':e.col;
      cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy+bob,e.sz*0.42*eliteScale,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
    }
    // HP bar
    if(e.hp<e.mhp||e.isDungeonElite){
      const bw=e.sz*(e.isDungeonElite?2.3:1),bh=e.isDungeonElite?7:4;
      const barY=sy-e.sz*0.65*eliteScale;
      cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.78)';
      cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,barY,bw,bh);
      cx.fillStyle=e.isDungeonElite?'#b25cff':'#ff3333';
      cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,barY,bw*Math.max(0,e.hp/e.mhp),bh);
      if(e.isDungeonElite){cx.strokeStyle='#f2c45e';cx.lineWidth=1;cx.strokeRect(sx-bw/2,barY,bw,bh)}
    }
    // Уровень и имя моба
    if(e.lvl&&e.type&&e.type.n){
      cx.textAlign='center';
      cx.font=e.isDungeonElite?'bold 11px monospace':'bold 9px monospace';
      const _eliteName=(e.isDungeonElite?'⚠ ЭЛИТА · ':'')+'['+e.lvl+'] '+e.type.n;
      const _nameY=sy-e.sz*0.75*eliteScale;
      cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.82)';
      cx.fillText(_eliteName,sx+1,_nameY+1);
      cx.fillStyle=e.isDungeonElite?'#ffd76a':'#ffdd88';
      cx.fillText(_eliteName,sx,_nameY);
      cx.textAlign='left';
    }
  }

  // Crystal projectiles from the world boss.
  if(P.scene==='worldboss')drawWorldCrystalProjectiles();

  // Boss fireballs
  for(const f of PR){
    const fx=f.x-cam.x,fy=f.y-cam.y;
    const fg=cx.createRadialGradient(fx,fy,0,fx,fy,15);
    fg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,240,180,0.95)');
    fg.addColorStop(0.4,'rgba(255,140,40,0.75)');
    fg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,60,0,0)');
    cx.fillStyle=fg;cx.beginPath();cx.arc(fx,fy,15,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  }

  // Online players from Realtime Presence (same scene only).
  drawOnlinePlayers();

  // Equipped visuals.
  updatePetFollower();
  drawPetFollower();

  // Wings and cloak stay as equipment/craft items,
  // but are NOT rendered in the world.
  // Player is always rendered in the normal world pass.
  // The old reward-only late-render branch could make the hero invisible
  // between Titan death and personal-chest pickup on some mobile browsers.
  drawPlayer();

  // Player projectiles are rendered above world sprites so shots stay readable.
  try{drawPlayerCannonballs()}catch(_){}
  try{drawPlayerArrows()}catch(_){}

  // Artifact floats above/beside the character.
  drawEquippedArtifact();

  // V104 depth for the original Phoenix and all ordinary dungeon/arena mobs.
  // When the player walks behind their ground shadow, their sprite body is redrawn on top.
  ppaDrawWorldBodyOccluders();

  // V89 depth sorting for the large clan boss.
  // If the player's feet are behind the boss, the boss body covers the player.
  if(P.scene==='clanboss1'){
    const _depthBoss=clanBossCombatTarget();
    if(_depthBoss)drawClanBossBodyOccluder(_depthBoss);
  }

  // World boss depth: player disappears behind the Titan when walking behind its body.
  if(P.scene==='worldboss'){
    const _worldDepthBoss=worldCrystalBossEntity();
    if(_worldDepthBoss)drawWorldCrystalBossOccluder(_worldDepthBoss);
  }

  // V204: small frameless character name follows the hero in world space.
  try{drawPlayerNickname()}catch(_){}

  drawMinimap();

  // Return to physical screen coordinates for screen-space effects.
  cx.restore();

  // Vignette — cached until the canvas size changes.
  cx.fillStyle=ppaGetVignetteGradient();cx.fillRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
}

function drawNPC(){
  const t=Date.now()*0.003;
  const groundDrop=12; // visually seat every NPC on the town floor
  for(const npc of ((SCENES[P.scene]&&SCENES[P.scene].npcs)||[])){
    if(npc.hidden)continue;
    const nx=npc.x-cam.x,ny=npc.y-cam.y;
    if(nx<-220||ny<-260||nx>cv.width+220||ny>cv.height+220)continue;
    const gy=ny+groundDrop;
    const glow=Math.sin(t*3+npc.x*0.001)*0.12+0.88;

    // Dark contact shadow so the NPC base visibly touches the ground.
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.30)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(nx,gy+15,47,13,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    // Small interaction glow directly on the ground.
    const g=cx.createRadialGradient(nx,gy,0,nx,gy,70);
    g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,190,70,'+(0.18*glow)+')');
    g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,190,70,0)');
    cx.fillStyle=g;cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(nx,gy+8,70,28,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    // Actual supplied NPC art, lowered to a common ground baseline.
    if(npc.img&&npc.img.complete&&npc.img.naturalWidth>0){
      const dh=npc.id==='dungeon'?126:(npc.id==='fartzone_guide'?90:112);
      const dw=dh*(npc.img.naturalWidth/npc.img.naturalHeight);
      cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
      cx.drawImage(npc.img,nx-dw/2,gy-dh+18,dw,dh);
    }else if(npc.id==='clan'){
      // Temporary in-game pixel silhouette until the dedicated Clan Master sprite is approved.
      cx.save();
      cx.translate(Math.round(nx),Math.round(gy));
      cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
      cx.fillStyle='#151116';cx.fillRect(-22,-84,44,70);
      cx.fillStyle='#2b1718';cx.fillRect(-17,-72,34,50);
      cx.fillStyle='#9a6b2d';cx.fillRect(-3,-63,6,34);
      cx.fillStyle='#c89642';cx.fillRect(-8,-50,16,4);
      cx.fillStyle='#241b18';cx.fillRect(-14,-101,28,23);
      cx.fillStyle='#0a090b';cx.fillRect(-10,-96,20,18);
      cx.fillStyle='#d0a452';cx.fillRect(-4,-91,3,3);cx.fillRect(2,-91,3,3);
      cx.fillStyle='#7c5327';cx.fillRect(-26,-72,8,48);cx.fillRect(18,-72,8,48);
      cx.fillStyle='#c8953c';cx.font='bold 19px serif';cx.textAlign='center';cx.fillText('⚜',0,-28);
      cx.restore();
    }

    cx.fillStyle='rgba(255,220,120,'+glow+')';
    cx.font='bold 12px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';
    cx.fillText('▶ '+npc.label,nx,gy-158);
    cx.textAlign='left';
  }
}
function drawTeleport(){
  const tp=SCENES.safe.teleport;
  const tx=tp.x-cam.x,ty=tp.y-cam.y;
  const t=Date.now()*0.002;
  const spin=Date.now()*0.004;

  // Outer glow
  const g=cx.createRadialGradient(tx,ty,0,tx,ty,tp.r*2.2);
  g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(80,180,255,0.35)');
  g.addColorStop(0.5,'rgba(40,100,200,0.15)');
  g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(20,60,150,0)');
  cx.fillStyle=g;
  cx.beginPath();cx.arc(tx,ty,tp.r*2.2,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Rotating rune rings
  cx.save();cx.translate(tx,ty);
  for(let i=0;i<2;i++){
    cx.save();
    cx.rotate(spin*(i===0?1:-1.3));
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba('+(100+i*40)+','+(180+i*30)+',255,'+(0.7-i*0.2)+')';
    cx.lineWidth=3-i;
    cx.setLineDash([12,8]);
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(0,0,tp.r*(0.7+i*0.3),0,Math.PI*2);cx.stroke();
    cx.setLineDash([]);
    // Rune dots on the ring
    for(let a=0;a<6;a++){
      const ang=a*Math.PI/3;
      cx.fillStyle='rgba(120,200,255,'+(0.6-i*0.2)+')';
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.arc(Math.cos(ang)*tp.r*(0.7+i*0.3),Math.sin(ang)*tp.r*(0.7+i*0.3),3-i,0,Math.PI*2);
      cx.fill();
    }
    cx.restore();
  }
  // Swirling center
  for(let i=0;i<8;i++){
    const a=i*Math.PI/4+spin*2;
    const r=tp.r*0.5*(0.5+Math.sin(t*2+i)*0.3);
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(150,210,255,'+(0.3+Math.sin(t*3+i)*0.2)+')';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(Math.cos(a)*r,Math.sin(a)*r,4,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  }
  cx.restore();

  // Label
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(120,200,255,0.8)';
  cx.font='bold 13px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.fillText(tp.label,tx,ty-tp.r-12);
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(150,150,150,0.5)';cx.font='8px monospace';
  cx.fillText('Вход в подземелье',tx,ty-tp.r-26);
  cx.textAlign='left';
}

function drawPortal(){
  const p=SCENES[P.scene].portal;
  const px=p.x-cam.x,py=p.y-cam.y;
  const t=Date.now()*0.003;
  const pulse=Math.sin(t*2)*0.1+0.9;

  // Outer glow
  const g=cx.createRadialGradient(px,py,0,px,py,p.r*2);
  g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(100,60,255,0.4)');
  g.addColorStop(0.5,'rgba(80,40,200,0.2)');
  g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(60,20,150,0)');
  cx.fillStyle=g;
  cx.beginPath();cx.arc(px,py,p.r*2,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Portal swirl rings
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
    const r=p.r*(0.5+i*0.25)*pulse;
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba('+ (140+i*30) +','+ (80+i*40) +',255,'+(0.6-i*0.15)+')';
    cx.lineWidth=3-i;
    cx.beginPath();
    for(let a=0;a<Math.PI*2;a+=0.1){
      const wobble=Math.sin(a*5+t+i)*4;
      const rx=r+wobble;
      const x=px+Math.cos(a+t*(1+i*0.3))*rx;
      const y=py+Math.sin(a+t*(1+i*0.3))*rx;
      if(a===0)cx.moveTo(x,y);else cx.lineTo(x,y);
    }
    cx.closePath();cx.stroke();
  }

  // Center glow
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(180,120,255,0.3)';
  cx.beginPath();cx.arc(px,py,p.r*0.4,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Label
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(200,160,255,0.9)';
  cx.font='bold 14px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.fillText('► '+p.label,px,py-p.r-18);
  if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(230,210,255,0.72)';cx.font='bold 10px monospace';
    cx.fillText('ТЕЛЕПОРТ В МИРНЫЙ ГОРОД',px,py-p.r-34);
  }
  cx.textAlign='left';
}


var PET_FOLLOW={x:0,y:0,scene:'',phase:0,dir:'S'};

function resetPetFollower(){
  PET_FOLLOW.x=P.x-(P.face||1)*50;PET_FOLLOW.y=P.y+22;PET_FOLLOW.scene=P.scene;PET_FOLLOW.phase=0;PET_FOLLOW.dir='S';
}
function updatePetFollower(){
  var it=INV&&INV.equipped?INV.equipped.pet:null;if(!it)return;
  if(PET_FOLLOW.scene!==P.scene||Math.hypot(PET_FOLLOW.x-P.x,PET_FOLLOW.y-P.y)>330)resetPetFollower();
  var mv=Math.hypot(P.vx||0,P.vy||0),dxn,dyn;
  if(mv>.15){dxn=(P.vx||0)/mv;dyn=(P.vy||0)/mv}
  else{var pf=playerFacing4();dxn=pf==='E'?1:pf==='W'?-1:0;dyn=pf==='S'?1:pf==='N'?-1:0}
  var tx=P.x-dxn*52-dyn*16,ty=P.y-dyn*52+dxn*16+10;
  var dx=tx-PET_FOLLOW.x,dy=ty-PET_FOLLOW.y,dd=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
  var step=Math.min(dd,Math.max(1.7,Math.min(6.3,dd*.11)));
  if(dd>1){PET_FOLLOW.x+=dx/dd*step;PET_FOLLOW.y+=dy/dd*step;
    if(Math.abs(dy)>Math.abs(dx))PET_FOLLOW.dir=dy>0?'S':'N';else PET_FOLLOW.dir=d

function drawTeleport(){
  const tp=SCENES.safe.teleport;
  const tx=tp.x-cam.x,ty=tp.y-cam.y;
  const t=Date.now()*0.002;
  const spin=Date.now()*0.004;

  // Outer glow
  const g=cx.createRadialGradient(tx,ty,0,tx,ty,tp.r*2.2);
  g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(80,180,255,0.35)');
  g.addColorStop(0.5,'rgba(40,100,200,0.15)');
  g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(20,60,150,0)');
  cx.fillStyle=g;
  cx.beginPath();cx.arc(tx,ty,tp.r*2.2,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Rotating rune rings
  cx.save();cx.translate(tx,ty);
  for(let i=0;i<2;i++){
    cx.save();
    cx.rotate(spin*(i===0?1:-1.3));
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba('+(100+i*40)+','+(180+i*30)+',255,'+(0.7-i*0.2)+')';
    cx.lineWidth=3-i;
    cx.setLineDash([12,8]);
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(0,0,tp.r*(0.7+i*0.3),0,Math.PI*2);cx.stroke();
    cx.setLineDash([]);
    // Rune dots on the ring
    for(let a=0;a<6;a++){
      const ang=a*Math.PI/3;
      cx.fillStyle='rgba(120,200,255,'+(0.6-i*0.2)+')';
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.arc(Math.cos(ang)*tp.r*(0.7+i*0.3),Math.sin(ang)*tp.r*(0.7+i*0.3),3-i,0,Math.PI*2);
      cx.fill();
    }
    cx.restore();
  }
  // Swirling center
  for(let i=0;i<8;i++){
    const a=i*Math.PI/4+spin*2;
    const r=tp.r*0.5*(0.5+Math.sin(t*2+i)*0.3);
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(150,210,255,'+(0.3+Math.sin(t*3+i)*0.2)+')';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(Math.cos(a)*r,Math.sin(a)*r,4,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  }
  cx.restore();

  // Label
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(120,200,255,0.8)';
  cx.font='bold 13px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.fillText(tp.label,tx,ty-tp.r-12);
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(150,150,150,0.5)';cx.font='8px monospace';
  cx.fillText('Вход в подземелье',tx,ty-tp.r-26);
  cx.textAlign='left';
}

function drawPortal(){
  const p=SCENES[P.scene].portal;
  const px=p.x-cam.x,py=p.y-cam.y;
  const t=Date.now()*0.003;
  const pulse=Math.sin(t*2)*0.1+0.9;

  // Outer glow
  const g=cx.createRadialGradient(px,py,0,px,py,p.r*2);
  g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(100,60,255,0.4)');
  g.addColorStop(0.5,'rgba(80,40,200,0.2)');
  g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(60,20,150,0)');
  cx.fillStyle=g;
  cx.beginPath();cx.arc(px,py,p.r*2,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Portal swirl rings
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
    const r=p.r*(0.5+i*0.25)*pulse;
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba('+ (140+i*30) +','+ (80+i*40) +',255,'+(0.6-i*0.15)+')';
    cx.lineWidth=3-i;
    cx.beginPath();
    for(let a=0;a<Math.PI*2;a+=0.1){
      const wobble=Math.sin(a*5+t+i)*4;
      const rx=r+wobble;
      const x=px+Math.cos(a+t*(1+i*0.3))*rx;
      const y=py+Math.sin(a+t*(1+i*0.3))*rx;
      if(a===0)cx.moveTo(x,y);else cx.lineTo(x,y);
    }
    cx.closePath();cx.stroke();
  }

  // Center glow
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(180,120,255,0.3)';
  cx.beginPath();cx.arc(px,py,p.r*0.4,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Label
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(200,160,255,0.9)';
  cx.font='bold 14px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.fillText('► '+p.label,px,py-p.r-18);
  if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(230,210,255,0.72)';cx.font='bold 10px monospace';
    cx.fillText('ТЕЛЕПОРТ В МИРНЫЙ ГОРОД',px,py-p.r-34);
  }
  cx.textAlign='left';
}


var PET_FOLLOW={x:0,y:0,scene:'',phase:0,dir:'S'};

function resetPetFollower(){
  PET_FOLLOW.x=P.x-(P.face||1)*50;PET_FOLLOW.y=P.y+22;PET_FOLLOW.scene=P.scene;PET_FOLLOW.phase=0;PET_FOLLOW.dir='S';
}
function updatePetFollower(){
  var it=INV&&INV.equipped?INV.equipped.pet:null;if(!it)return;
  if(PET_FOLLOW.scene!==P.scene||Math.hypot(PET_FOLLOW.x-P.x,PET_FOLLOW.y-P.y)>330)resetPetFollower();
  var mv=Math.hypot(P.vx||0,P.vy||0),dxn,dyn;
  if(mv>.15){dxn=(P.vx||0)/mv;dyn=(P.vy||0)/mv}
  else{var pf=playerFacing4();dxn=pf==='E'?1:pf==='W'?-1:0;dyn=pf==='S'?1:pf==='N'?-1:0}
  var tx=P.x-dxn*52-dyn*16,ty=P.y-dyn*52+dxn*16+10;
  var dx=tx-PET_FOLLOW.x,dy=ty-PET_FOLLOW.y,dd=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
  var step=Math.min(dd,Math.max(1.7,Math.min(6.3,dd*.11)));
  if(dd>1){PET_FOLLOW.x+=dx/dd*step;PET_FOLLOW.y+=dy/dd*step;
    if(Math.abs(dy)>Math.abs(dx))PET_FOLLOW.dir=dy>0?'S':'N';else PET_FOLLOW.dir=dx>0?'E':'W'}
  PET_FOLLOW.phase+=(dd>1.2?.34:.09);
}

  if(!src)return null;if(!getCachedImage.cache)getCachedImage.cache={};var c
===== fillText( #26 @ 97890205 =====
);
      cx.fillStyle='#9a6b2d';cx.fillRect(-3,-63,6,34);
      cx.fillStyle='#c89642';cx.fillRect(-8,-50,16,4);
      cx.fillStyle='#241b18';cx.fillRect(-14,-101,28,23);
      cx.fillStyle='#0a090b';cx.fillRect(-10,-96,20,18);
      cx.fillStyle='#d0a452';cx.fillRect(-4,-91,3,3);cx.fillRect(2,-91,3,3);
      cx.fillStyle='#7c5327';cx.fillRect(-26,-72,8,48);cx.fillRect(18,-72,8,48);
      cx.fillStyle='#c8953c';cx.font='bold 19px serif';cx.textAlign='center';cx.fillText('⚜',0,-28);
      cx.restore();
    }

    cx.fillStyle='rgba(255,220,120,'+glow+')';
    cx.font='bold 12px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';
    cx.fillText('▶ '+npc.label,nx,gy-158);
    cx.textAlign='left';
  }
}

function getCachedImage(src){
  if(!src)return null;if(!getCachedImage.cache)getCachedImage.cache={};var c=getCachedImage.cache;
  if(!c[src]){var im=new Image();im.src=src;c[src]=im}return c[src];
}

  var step=Math.min(dd,Math.max(1.7,Math.min(6.3,dd*.11)));
  if(dd>1){PET_FOLLOW.x+=dx/dd*step;PET_FOLLOW.y+=dy/dd*step;
    if(Math.abs(dy)>Math.abs(dx))PET_FOLLOW.dir=dy>0?'S':'N';else PET_FOLLOW.dir=dx>0?'E':'W'}
  PET_FOLLOW.phase+=(dd>1.2?.34:.09);
}
function getCachedImage(src){
  if(!src)return null;if(!getCachedImage.cache)getCachedImage.cache={};var c=getCachedImage.cache;
  if(!c[src]){var im=new Image();im.src=src;c[src]=im}return c[src];
}

      cx.arc(Math.cos(ang)*tp.r*(0.7+i*0.3),Math.sin(ang)*tp.r*(0.7+i*0.3),3-i,0,Math.PI*2);
      cx.fill();
    }
    cx.restore();
  }
  // Swirling center
  for(let i=0;i<8;i++){
    const a=i*Math.PI/4+spin*2;
    const r=tp.r*0.5*(0.5+Math.sin(t*2+i)*0.3);
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(150,210,255,'+(0.3+Math.sin(t*3+i)*0.2)+')';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(Math.cos(a)*r,Math.sin(a)*r,4,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  }

  // Label

  cx.fillText('Вход в подземелье',tx,ty-tp.r-26);
  cx.textAlign='left';
}

function drawPortal(){
  const p=SCENES[P.scene].portal;
  const px=p.x-cam.x,py=p.y-cam.y;
  const t=Date.now()*0.003;
  const pulse=Math.sin(t*2)*0.1+0.9;

  // Outer glow
  const g=cx.createRadialGradient(px,py,0,px,py,p.r*2);
  g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(100,60,255,0.4)');
  g.addColorStop(0.5,'rgba(80,40,200,0.2)');
  g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(60,20,150,0)');
  cx.fillStyle=g;
  cx.beginPath();cx.arc(px,py,p.r*2,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Portal swirl rings
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
    const r=p.r*(0.5+i*0.25)*pulse;
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba('+ (140+i*30) +','+ (80+i*40) +',255,'+(0.6-i*0.15)+')';
    cx.lineWidth=3-i;
    cx.beginPath();
    for(let a=0;a<Math.PI*2;a+=0.1){
      const wobble=Math.sin(a*5+t+i)*4;
      const rx=r+wobble;
      const x=px+Math.cos(a+t*(1+i*0.3))*rx;
      const y=py+Math.sin(a+t*(1+i*0.3))*rx;
      if(a===0)cx.moveTo(x,y);else cx.lineTo(x,y);
    }
    cx.closePath();cx.stroke();
  }

  // Center glow
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(180,120,255,0.3)';
  cx.beginPath();cx.arc(px,py,p.r*0.4,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Label
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(200,160,255,0.9)';
  cx.font='bold 14px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.fillText('► '+p.label,px,py-p.r-18);
  if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(230,210,255,0.72)';cx.font='bold 10px monospace';
    cx.fillText('ТЕЛЕПОРТ В МИРНЫЙ ГОРОД',px,py-p.r-34);
  }
  cx.textAlign='left';
}


var PET_FOLLOW={x:0,y:0,scene:'',phase:0,dir:'S'};

function resetPetFollower(){
  PET_FOLLOW.x=P.x-(P.face||1)*50;PET_FOLLOW.y=P.y+22;PET_FOLLOW.scene=P.scene;PET_FOLLOW.phase=0;PET_FOLLOW.dir='S';
}
function updatePetFollower(){
  var it=INV&&INV.equipped?INV.equipped.pet:null;if(!it)return;
  if(PET_FOLLOW.scene!==P.scene||Math.hypot(PET_FOLLOW.x-P.x,PET_FOLLOW.y-P.y)>330)resetPetFollower();
  var mv=Math.hypot(P.vx||0,P.vy||0),dxn,dyn;
  if(mv>.15){dxn=(P.vx||0)/mv;dyn=(P.vy||0)/mv}
  else{var pf=playerFacing4();dxn=pf==='E'?1:pf==='W'?-1:0;dyn=pf==='S'?1:pf==='N'?-1:0}
  var tx=P.x-dxn*52-dyn*16,ty=P.y-dyn*52+dxn*16+10;
  var dx=tx-PET_FOLLOW.x,dy=ty-PET_FOLLOW.y,dd=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
  var step=Math.min(dd,Math.max(1.7,Math.min(6.3,dd*.11)));
  if(dd>1){PET_FOLLOW.x+=dx/dd*step;PET_FOLLOW.y+=dy/dd*step;
    if(Math.abs(dy)>Math.abs(dx))PET_FOLLOW.dir=dy>0?'S':'N';else PET_FOLLOW.dir=dx>0?'E':'W'}
  PET_FOLLOW.phase+=(dd>1.2?.34:.09);
}
function getCachedImage(src){
  if(!src)return null;if(!getCachedImage.cache)getCachedImage.cache={};var c=getCachedImage.cache;
  if(!c[src]){var im=new Image();im.src=src;c[src]=im}return c[src];
}

  const pulse=Math.sin(t*2)*0.1+0.9;

  g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(100,60,255,0.4)');
  g.addColorStop(0.5,'rgba(80,40,200,0.2)');
  g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(60,20,150,0)');

  cx.beginPath();cx.arc(px,py,p.r*2,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    const r=p.r*(0.5+i*0.25)*pulse;

    cx.lineWidth=3-i;

      const wobble=Math.sin(a*5+t+i)*4;
      const rx=r+wobble;

      const y=py+Math.sin(a+t*(1+i*0.3))*rx;
      if(a===0)cx.moveTo(x,y);else cx.lineTo(x,y);
    }
    cx.closePath();cx.stroke();
  }

  // Center glow
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(180,120,255,0.3)';
  cx.beginPath();cx.arc(px,py,p.r*0.4,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Label
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(200,160,255,0.9)';

    cx.fillStyle='rgba(230,210,255,0.72)';cx.font='bold 10px monospace';
    cx.fillText('ТЕЛЕПОРТ В МИРНЫЙ ГОРОД',px,py-p.r-34);
  }
  cx.textAlign='left';
