// V231 HOTFIX: main pet renderer uses PET_DIR_ART directly; PET_IMG belongs to blacksmith iframe scope.

function drawPetFollower(){
  var it=INV&&INV.equipped?INV.equipped.pet:null;if(!it)return;
  var sx=PET_FOLLOW.x-cam.x,sy=PET_FOLLOW.y-cam.y;
  var moving=Math.hypot(P.vx||0,P.vy||0)>.15,hop=moving?Math.abs(Math.sin(PET_FOLLOW.phase))*5:Math.sin(PET_FOLLOW.phase)*1.2;
  var glow=RCOL_P[it.rarity]||'#bbbbbb';
  cx.save();cx.globalAlpha=.28;cx.fillStyle=glow;cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+7,11,4,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  cx.globalAlpha=.42;cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.7)';cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+9,9,3,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();cx.globalAlpha=1;
  var sprites=it.dirSprites||PET_DIR_ART[it.petName||it.name]||null,src=sprites?(sprites[PET_FOLLOW.dir]||sprites.S):it.img,im=getCachedImage(src);
  if(im&&im.complete&&im.naturalWidth>0){
    var sizeBonus=(it.stellarGuardian||it.petName===STELLAR_GUARDIAN_NAME)?6:0;
    var h=30+sizeBonus,w=h*(im.naturalWidth/im.naturalHeight);
    w=Math.min(38+sizeBonus,Math.max(20+sizeBonus,w));
    cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;cx.drawImage(im,sx-w/2,sy-h+7-hop,w,h)}
  else{cx.font='18px serif';cx.textAlign='center';cx.fillText('🐾',sx,sy-hop);cx.textAlign='left'}cx.restore();
}
function drawEquippedWings(){
  var it=INV&&INV.equipped?INV.equipped.wings:null;
  if(!it)return;

  var rarity=it.rarity||'rare';
  var sprites=it.dirSprites||WING_DIR_ART[rarity]||WING_DIR_ART.rare;
  var dir=playerFacing4();

  // Берём стабильный полный спрайт крыльев.
  var fullSrc=(sprites&&sprites.N)||(sprites&&sprites.S)||it.img;
  var im=getCachedImage(fullSrc);
  if(!im||!im.complete||!im.naturalWidth)return;

  var sx=P.x-cam.x;
  var sy=P.y-cam.y;
  var bob=Math.sin(P.bob)*1.8;

  cx.save();
  cx.globalAlpha=.96;
  cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;

  if(dir==='N'){
    // Вверх: оба крыла полностью за спиной.
    var h=76;
    var w=h*(im.naturalWidth/im.naturalHeight);
    w=Math.min(102,Math.max(72,w));
    cx.drawImage(im,sx-w/2,sy-h+26+bob,w,h);
  }
  else if(dir==='S'){
    // Вниз: оба крыла остаются за персонажем.
    // Центральную часть дополнительно перекроет тело персонажа.
    var h=68;
    var w=h*(im.naturalWidth/im.naturalHeight);
    w=Math.min(110,Math.max(84,w));
    cx.drawImage(im,sx-w/2,sy-h+30+bob,w,h);
  }
  else{
    // БОКОВОЙ ВИД:
    // показываем только одно крыло и НЕ разрешаем ему рисоваться внутри центра персонажа.
    var sw=im.naturalWidth/2;
    var sh=im.naturalHeight;
    var h=66;
    var w=h*(sw/sh);
    w=Math.min(50,Math.max(34,w));

    if(dir==='E'){
      // Персонаж бежит вправо:
      // одно крыло полностью слева от корпуса.
      cx.save();
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.rect(-10000,-10000,sx-18+10000,20000);
      cx.clip();
      cx.drawImage(
        im,
        0,0,sw,sh,
        sx-w-18,sy-h+28+bob,w,h
      );
      cx.restore();
    }else{
      // Персонаж бежит влево:
      // одно крыло полностью справа от корпуса.
      cx.save();
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.rect(sx+18,-10000,10000,20000);
      cx.clip();
      cx.drawImage(
        im,
        sw,0,sw,sh,
        sx+18,sy-h+28+bob,w,h
      );
      cx.restore();
    }
  }

  cx.restore();
}
function drawEquippedCloak(){
  var it=INV&&INV.equipped?INV.equipped.cloak:null;if(!it)return;
  var dir=playerFacing4(),sx=P.x-cam.x,sy=P.y-cam.y,c=RCOL_P[it.rarity]||'#777',bob=Math.sin(P.bob)*2;
  cx.save();cx.translate(sx,sy+bob);cx.globalAlpha=.88;cx.fillStyle=c;cx.strokeStyle='rgba(20,15,20,.8)';cx.lineWidth=2;cx.beginPath();
  if(dir==='N'){cx.moveTo(-18,-29);cx.lineTo(18,-29);cx.lineTo(24,25);cx.lineTo(0,37);cx.lineTo(-24,25)}
  else if(dir==='S'){cx.moveTo(-14,-18);cx.lineTo(14,-18);cx.lineTo(18,22);cx.lineTo(0,30);cx.lineTo(-18,22)}
  else if(dir==='E'){cx.moveTo(-8,-24);cx.lineTo(13,-20);cx.lineTo(23,25);cx.lineTo(3,31);cx.lineTo(-10,18)}
  else{cx.moveTo(8,-24);cx.lineTo(-13,-20);cx.lineTo(-23,25);cx.lineTo(-3,31);cx.lineTo(10,18)}
  cx.closePath();cx.fill();cx.stroke();cx.globalAlpha=.45;cx.fillStyle='#fff';cx.beginPath();cx.arc(0,-21,4,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();cx.restore();
}
function drawEquippedArtifact(){
  var it=INV&&INV.equipped?INV.equipped.artifact:null;if(!it)return;
  var im=getCachedImage(it.img||ARTIFACT_ICON_DATA),t=Date.now()*.002,sx=P.x-cam.x+Math.cos(t)*28,sy=P.y-cam.y-36+Math.sin(t*1.3)*7;
  var c=RCOL_P[it.rarity]||'#c990ff';cx.save();cx.globalAlpha=.25;cx.fillStyle=c;cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy,15,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();cx.globalAlpha=1;
  if(im&&im.complete&&im.naturalWidth>0){cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;cx.drawImage(im,sx-12,sy-12,24,24)}
  else{cx.fillStyle=c;cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy,6,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill()}cx.restore();
}


// === V204 NICKNAME ABOVE PLAYER ===========================================
function ppaPlayerNickname(){
  try{
    var n=(typeof INV!=='undefined'&&INV&&String(INV.playerName||'').trim())
      ||String(window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||'').trim();
    return n||'';
  }catch(_){return String(window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||'').trim()}
}
function ppaPlayerVisualTopScreenY(){
  var sy=P.y-cam.y;
  var _sv=P.scene==='clansiege'?CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE:1;
  var bob=Math.sin(P.bob)*(playerUsesGnomeSprites()?2.0:3)*_sv;
  var dh,dy;
  if(playerUsesGnomeSprites()){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*GNOME_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(113/128)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesArcherSprites()){
    dh=phoneChara
===== fillText( #31 @ 98222686 =====
playerUsesGnomeSprites()?2.0:3)*_sv;
  var dh,dy;
  if(playerUsesGnomeSprites()){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*GNOME_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(113/128)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesArcherSprites()){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*ARCHER_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(184/224)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&ASSASSIN_ANIM[P.anim]){
    var sc=(P.anim==='idle')?ASSASSIN_IDLE_DRAW_SCALE:(P.anim==='attack'?ASSASSIN_ATTACK_DRAW_SCALE:ASSASSIN_RUN_DRAW_SCALE);
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*sc,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(156/192)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesTankSprites()&&TANK_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*TANK_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(232/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&BERSERKER_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*BERSERKER_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(232/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesPriestSprites()&&PRIEST_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*PRIEST_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(236/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesMageSprites()&&MAGE_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*MAGE_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(236/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesPaladinSprites()&&PALADIN_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*PALADIN_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(234/256)*dh;
  }else{
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*1.4,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-dh;
  }
  return sy+(bob+dy)*_sv;
}
function ppaPlayerClanName(){
  try{
    var c=(typeof CLAN_LOCAL_STATE!=='undefined'&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan)
      ?String(CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan.name||'').trim():'';
    return c;
  }catch(_){return ''}
}
function drawPlayerNickname(){
  var name=ppaPlayerNickname();
  if(!name)return;
  var clan=ppaPlayerClanName();
  var sx=P.x-cam.x;
  var y=ppaPlayerVisualTopScreenY()-9;

  cx.save();
  cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.textBaseline='bottom';
  cx.lineJoin='round';

  if(clan){
    var clanText='['+clan.slice(0,18)+']';
    cx.font='700 8px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
    cx.lineWidth=2.4;
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba(8,12,20,.94)';
    cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.78)';
    cx.shadowBlur=3;
    cx.strokeText(clanText,sx,y-12);
    cx.shadowBlur=0;
    cx.fillStyle='#a9cfff';
    cx.fillText(clanText,sx,y-12);
  }

  cx.font='600 10px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
  cx.lineWidth=2.6;
  cx.strokeStyle='rgba(18,8,5,.92)';
  cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.72)';
  cx.shadowBlur=3;
  cx.strokeText(name.slice(0,18),sx,y);
  cx.shadowBlur=0;
  cx.fillStyle='#f2d39a';
  cx.fillText(name.slice(0,18),sx,y);
  cx.restore();
}
// ==========================================================================

function drawPlayer(){
  const sx=P.x-cam.x,sy=P.y-cam.y;
  const siegeVisualScale=P.scene==='clansiege'?CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE:1;

  // V277: only the player's 8 classes are drawn 10 px smaller.
  // No collision, camera, NPC, mob, AI or combat values are changed.
  const PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX=10;
  const playerClassDrawHeight=(baseHeight)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);
    return Math.max(8,phoneCharacterDrawHeight(baseHeight,siegeVisualScale)-PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX/sc);
  };
  const playerClassBodySize=(baseSize)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);
    return Math.max(8,phoneCharacterBodySize(baseSize,siegeVisualScale)-PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX/sc);
  };
  const isGnome=playerUsesGnomeSprites();
  const isArcher=playerUsesArcherSprites();
  const isAssassinCustom=playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&!!ASSASSIN_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isTank=playerUsesTankSprites()&&!!TANK_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isBerserker=playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&!!BERSERKER_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isPriest=playerUsesPriestSprites()&&!!PRIEST_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isMage=playerUsesMageSprites()&&!!MAGE_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isPaladin=playerUsesPaladinSprites()&&!!PALADIN_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isCustomDirectional=isGnome||isArcher||isAssassinCustom||isTank||isBerserker||isPriest||isMage||isPaladin;
  const phoneBodyBase=playerClassBodySize(P.sz);
  const visualBody=(isGnome?phoneBodyBase*0.72:phoneBodyBase)*siegeVisualScale;
  const bob=P.scene==='fartzone'?0:Math.sin(P.bob)*(isGnome?2.0:3);

  // Shadow follows the visible character size.
  // Fart Zone: exact feet-to-ground anchor, no visual hovering.
  const playerShadowY=P.scene==='fartzone'?sy+visualBody*0.40:sy+visualBody*0.45;
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.4)';
  cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,playerShadowY,visualBody*0.38,visualBody*0.14,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Dust when running
  if((Math.abs(P.vx)>0.5||Math.abs(P.vy)>0.5)&&Math.random()<0.25)
    PT.push({x:P.x+(Math.random()-.5)*(isGnome?6:12)*siegeVisualScale,y:P.y+visualBody*0.35,
      vx:-P.vx*0.4+(Math.random()-.5),vy:-P.vy*0.2+(Math.random()-.5)*0.5,
      life:10,ml:10,sz:(isGnome?1.5:2)*siegeVisualScale,col:'rgba(120,100,70,0.4)'});

  cx.save();
  cx.translate(sx,sy+bob);
  if(P.scene==='clansiege')cx.scale(CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE,CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE);

  // Legacy lean/recoil is disabled for the dwarf because his approved
  // 4-frame attack already contains the physical movement.
  if(!isCustomDirectional){
    let lean=0;
    if(P.shootT>0)lean=P.face>0?0.12:-0.12;
    const rc=P.recoil*6*(P.face>0?-1:1);
    cx.translate(rc,0);
    cx.rotate(lean);
  }

  const cur=playerAnimDef(P.anim);
  const canonicalDir=isCustomDirectional?playerDir8():0;
  const dirRow=isGnome
    ? GNOME_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
    : (isArcher
        ? ARCHER_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
        : (isAssassinCustom
            ? (P.anim==='run'
                ? ASSASSIN_RUN_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
                : ASSASSIN_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir])
            : (isTank
                ? TANK_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                : (isBerserker
                    ? BERSERKER_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                    : (isPriest
                        ? PRIEST_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                        : (isMage
                            ? MAGE_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                            : (isPaladin
                                ? PALADIN_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                                : (cur.rows ? getDirRow() : 0))))))));
  const useFlip=isGnome
    ? GNOME_FLIP_BY_DIR[canonicalDir]

function drawPlayerNickname(){

    cx.font='700 8px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
    cx.lineWidth=2.4;
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba(8,12,20,.94)';
    cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.78)';
    cx.shadowBlur=3;
    cx.strokeText(clanText,sx,y-12);
    cx.shadowBlur=0;
    cx.fillStyle='#a9cfff';
    cx.fillText(clanText,sx,y-12);
  }

  cx.font='600 10px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
  cx.lineWidth=2.6;
  cx.strokeStyle='rgba(18,8,5,.92)';
  cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.72)';

function drawPlayer(){
  const sx=P.x-cam.x,sy=P.y-cam.y;
  const siegeVisualScale=P.scene==='clansiege'?CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE:1;

  // V277: only the player's 8 classes are drawn 10 px smaller.
  // No collision, camera, NPC, mob, AI or combat values are changed.
  const PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX=10;
  const playerClassDrawHeight=(baseHeight)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);
    return Math.max(8,phoneCharacterDrawHeight(baseHeight,siegeVisualScale)-PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX/sc);
  };
  const playerClassBodySize=(baseSize)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);
    return Math.max(8,phoneCharacterBodySize(baseSize,siegeVisualScale)-PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX/sc);
  };
  const isGnome=playerUsesGnomeSprites();
  const isArcher=playerUsesArcherSprites();
  const isAssassinCustom=playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&!!ASSASSIN_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isTank=playerUsesTankSprites()&&!!TANK_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isBerserker=playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&!!BERSERKER_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isPriest=playerUsesPriestSprites()&&!!PRIEST_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isMage=playerUsesMageSprites()&&!!MAGE_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isPaladin=playerUsesPaladinSprites()&&!!PALADIN_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isCustomDirectional=isGnome||isArcher||isAssassinCustom||isTank||isBerserker||isPriest||isMage||isPaladin;
  const phoneBodyBase=playerClassBodySize(P.sz);
  const visualBody=(isGnome?phoneBodyBase*0.72:phoneBodyBase)*siegeVisualScale;
  const bob=P.scene==='fartzone'?0:Math.sin(P.bob)*(isGnome?2.0:3);

  // Shadow follows the visible character size.
  // Fart Zone: exact feet-to-ground anchor, no visual hovering.
  const playerShadowY=P.scene==='fartzone'?sy+visualBody*0.40:sy+visualBody*0.45;
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.4)';
  cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,playerShadowY,visualBody*0.38,visualBody*0.14,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Dust when running
  if((Math.abs(P.vx)>0.5||Math.abs(P.vy)>0.5)&&Math.random()<0.25)
    PT.push({x:P.x+(Math.random()-.5)*(isGnome?6:12)*siegeVisualScale,y:P.y+visualBody*0.35,
      vx:-P.vx*0.4+(Math.random()-.5),vy:-P.vy*0.2+(Math.random()-.5)*0.5,
      life:10,ml:10,sz:(isGnome?1.5:2)*siegeVisualScale,col:'rgba(120,100,70,0.4)'});

  cx.save();
  cx.translate(sx,sy+bob);
  if(P.scene==='clansiege')cx.scale(CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE,CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE);

  // Legacy lean/recoil is disabled for the dwarf because his approved
  // 4-frame attack already contains the physical movement.
  if(!isCustomDirectional){
    let lean=0;
    if(P.shootT>0)lean=P.face>0?0.12:-0.12;
    const rc=P.recoil*6*(P.face>0?-1:1);
    cx.translate(rc,0);
    cx.rotate(lean);
  }

  const cur=playerAnimDef(P.anim);
  const canonicalDir=isCustomDirectional?playerDir8():0;
  const dirRow=isGnome
    ? GNOME_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
    : (isArcher
        ? ARCHER_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
        : (isAssassinCustom
            ? (P.anim==='run'
                ? ASSASSIN_RUN_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
                : ASSASSIN_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir])
            : (isTank
                ? TANK_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                : (isBerserker
                    ? BERSERKER_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                    : (isPriest
                        ? PRIEST_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                        : (isMage
                            ? MAGE_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                            : (isPaladin
                                ? PALADIN_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                                : (cur.rows ? getDirRow() : 0))))))));
  const useFlip=isGnome
    ? GNOME_FLIP_BY_DIR[canonicalDir]
    : (isArcher
        ? ARCHER_FLIP_BY_DIR[canonicalDir]
        : ((isAssassinCustom||isTank||isBerserker||isPriest||isMage||isPaladin) ? false : (dirRow===2 && P.face>0)));
  const frameIndex=((P.animFrame%cur.frames)+cur.frames)%cur.frames;

  if(cur.img.complete && cur.img.naturalWidth > 0){
    const fh_row=cur.fh;
    const fw_frame=cur.fw;
    const footY=P.sz*0.40;
    const srcY=dirRow*fh_row;

    let dh,dw,dx,dy;

      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);

    }else if(isBerserker){

      dw=dh;

    }else if(isPriest){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*PRIEST_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(236/256)*dh);
    }else if(isMage){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*MAGE_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);

      cx.restore();
    }else{
      cx.drawImage(cur.img,fw_frame*frameIndex,srcY,fw_frame,fh_row,dx,dy,dw,dh);
    }
  } else {
    // Fallback while sprites load
    cx.fillStyle='#4488ff';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(0,0,phoneBodyBase*0.4,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
    cx.fillStyle='#fff';cx.font='10px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';
    cx.fillText('?',0,-2);
  }
  cx.restore();


  // Melee slash arc visual
  if(P.shootT>0&&P.meleeAng!==undefined&&P.attackMode==='melee'&&!playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&!playerUsesTankSprites()&&!playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&!playerUsesPaladinSprites()){
    const slashAlpha=P.shootT*0.6;
    cx.save();
    cx.translate(sx,sy-8+bob);
    cx.rotate(P.meleeAng);
    // Arc slash
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,200,'+slashAlpha+')';
    cx.lineWidth=4;
    cx.beginPath();

    cx.stroke();
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,'+slashAlpha*0.5+')';
    cx.lineWidth=2;
    cx.stroke();
    cx.restore();
  }
}

        '<span style="color:#ead6a7">['+(t.lvl||'?')+'] '+nm+'</span>';
  }else{
    out='<span style="color:#c6b99f">ПОДЗЕМЕЛЬЕ · ЦЕЛЬ НЕ ВЫБРАНА</span>';
  }
  if(out!==__v216DungeonHudLast){
    __v216DungeonHudLast=out;
    sm.innerHTML=out;
  }
  sm.style.display='block';
}

function v232Pct(ch){
  const p=Math.max(0,Number(ch)||0)*100;
  if(p===0)return '0%';
  const d=p>=10?1:(p>=1?2:(p>=.1?3:4));
  return p.toFixed(d).replace(/0+$/,'').replace(/\.$/,'')+'%';
}

  let sec=Math.max(0,Math.ceil((Number(ms)||0)/1000));
  const h=Math.floor(sec/3600);sec%=3600;
  const m=Math.floor(sec/60),ss=sec%60;
  return String(h).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(m).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(ss).padStart(2,'0');
};

function v232PushGear(e,rar){
  // Preserve the original 1–20 item power curve; 21+ content keeps the
  // established level-20 generation base used by V215.
  const genLvl=(e&&(e.dungeon21||e.isDungeon21Boss))?20:Math.max(1,Math.min(20,Number(e&&((e.roomLevel||e.lvl)))||20));
  const it=genItem(genLvl,false,rar);
  LOOT.push({x:e.x+(Math.random()-.5)*40,y:e.y+(Math.random()-.5)*40,kind:'gear',item:it,gear:it,bob:Math.random()*6});
}

    color:color||'#d69cff',rarity:'epic',amount:amount||1,bob:Math.random()*6
  });
}

function ppaPlayerClanName(){
  try{
    var c=(typeof CLAN_LOCAL_STATE!=='undefined'&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan)
      ?String(CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan.name||'').trim():'';
    return c;
  }catch(_){return ''}
}
function drawPlayerNickname(){
  var name=ppaPlayerNickname();
  if(!name)return;
  var clan=ppaPlayerClanName();
  var sx=P.x-cam.x;
  var y=ppaPlayerVisualTopScreenY()-9;

  cx.save();
  cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.textBaseline='bottom';
  cx.lineJoin='round';

  if(clan){
    var clanText='['+clan.slice(0,18)+']';
    cx.font='700 8px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
    cx.lineWidth=2.4;
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba(8,12,20,.94)';
    cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.78)';
    cx.shadowBlur=3;
    cx.strokeText(clanText,sx,y-12);
    cx.shadowBlur=0;
    cx.fillStyle='#a9cfff';
    cx.fillText(clanText,sx,y-12);
  }

  cx.font='600 10px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
  cx.lineWidth=2.6;
  cx.strokeStyle='rgba(18,8,5,.92)';
  cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.72)';
  cx.shadowBlur=3;
  cx.strokeText(name.slice(0,18),sx,y);
  cx.shadowBlur=0;
  cx.fillStyle='#f2d39a';
  cx.fillText(name.slice(0,18),sx,y);
  cx.restore();
}

function drawPlayer(){
  const sx=P.x-cam.x,sy=P.y-cam.y;
  const siegeVisualScale=P.scene==='clansiege'?CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE:1;

  // V277: only the player's 8 classes are drawn 10 px smaller.
  // No collision, camera, NPC, mob, AI or combat values are changed.
  const PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX=10;
  const playerClassDrawHeight=(baseHeight)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);
    return Math.max(8,phoneCharacterDrawHeight(baseHeight,siegeVisualScale)-PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX/sc);
  };
  const playerClassBodySize=(baseSize)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);
    return Math.max(8,phoneCharacterBodySize(baseSize,siegeVisualScale)-PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX/sc);
  };
  const isGnome=playerUsesGnomeSprites();
  const isArcher=playerUsesArcherSprites();
  const isAssassinCustom=playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&!!ASSASSIN_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isTank=playerUsesTankSprites()&&!!TANK_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isBerserker=playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&!!BERSERKER_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isPriest=playerUsesPriestSprites()&&!!PRIEST_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isMage=playerUsesMageSprites()&&!!MAGE_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isPaladin=playerUsesPaladinSprites()&&!!PALADIN_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isCustomDirectional=isGnome||isArcher||isAssassinCustom||isTank||isBerserker||isPriest||isMage||isPaladin;
  const phoneBodyBase=playerClassBodySize(P.sz);
  const visualBody=(isGnome?phoneBodyBase*0.72:phoneBodyBase)*siegeVisualScale;
  const bob=P.scene==='fartzone'?0:Math.sin(P.bob)*(isGnome?2.0:3);

  // Shadow follows the visible character size.
  // Fart Zone: exact feet-to-ground anchor, no visual hovering.
  const playerShadowY=P.scene==='fartzone'?sy+visualBody*0.40:sy+visualBody*0.45;
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.4)';
  cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,playerShadowY,visualBody*0.38,visualBody*0.14,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Dust when running
  if((Math.abs(P.vx)>0.5||Math.abs(P.vy)>0.5)&&Math.random()<0.25)
    PT.push({x:P.x+(Math.random()-.5)*(isGnome?6:12)*siegeVisualScale,y:P.y+visualBody*0.35,
      vx:-P.vx*0.4+(Math.random()-.5),vy:-P.vy*0.2+(Math.random()-.5)*0.5,
      life:10,ml:10,sz:(isGnome?1.5:2)*siegeVisualScale,col:'rgba(120,100,70,0.4)'});

  cx.save();
  cx.translate(sx,sy+bob);
  if(P.scene==='clansiege')cx.scale(CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE,CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE);

  // Legacy lean/recoil is disabled for the dwarf because his approved
  // 4-frame attack already contains the physical movement.
  if(!isCustomDirectional){
    let lean=0;
    if(P.shootT>0)lean=P.face>0?0.12:-0.12;
    const rc=P.recoil*6*(P.face>0?-1:1);
    cx.translate(rc,0);
    cx.rotate(lean);
  }

  const cur=playerAnimDef(P.anim);
  const canonicalDir=isCustomDirectional?playerDir8():0;
  const dirRow=isGnome
    ? GNOME_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
    : (isArcher
        ? ARCHER_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
        : (isAssassinCustom
            ? (P.anim==='run'
                ? ASSASSIN_RUN_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
                : ASSASSIN_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir])
            : (isTank
                ? TANK_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                : (isBerserker
                    ? BERSERKER_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                    : (isPriest
                        ? PRIEST_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                        : (isMage
                            ? MAGE_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                            : (isPaladin
                                ? PALADIN_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                                : (cur.rows ? getDirRow() : 0))))))));
  const useFlip=isGnome
    ? GNOME_FLIP_BY_DIR[canonicalDir]
    : (isArcher
        ? ARCHER_FLIP_BY_DIR[canonica
===== strokeText( #2 @ 98222892 =====
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*ARCHER_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(184/224)*dh;

  }else{
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*1.4,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-dh;
  }
  return sy+(bob+dy)*_sv;
}
